# BACKLOG.md — Отложенные задачи МастерРядом

> Этот файл — единое место для **всех** задач которые мы решили не делать сейчас.
> Каждый раз когда в обсуждении появляется новая deferred фича — добавляем сюда.
> Когда задача выполняется — переносится в «✅ Выполнено» с datestamp.

---

## ОРГАНИЗАЦИЯ

Задачи разделены по **категориям** и **приоритету**:
- 🔴 **Pre-launch blocker** — нельзя запустить production без этого
- 🟠 **High priority** — желательно до launch, можно сразу после
- 🟡 **Medium priority** — после launch когда будут реальные мастера
- 🔵 **Nice-to-have** — features которые приятно иметь, но не критичные

---

## 🎯 ТЕКУЩИЙ ФОКУС

**Платформа достроена** — redesign-фаза завершена (CHAT-UI-A 2026-05-19 закрыл sprint). Сейчас:
- 🧪 **Ручной QA pass** по 4 поверхностям (master cabinet / studio cabinet / client cabinet / public surfaces & widgets) — НЕ трогать тестируемый код
- 🔬 **Фоновый трек:** TEST-COVERAGE-A завершён (358/358 tests; +84 новых). Следующее фоновое — BOOKING-ENFORCEMENT после QA Этапа 2.1, потом multi-recipient notif после notif QA
- 🚀 **Перед launch:** проработать 🔴 PRE-LAUNCH BLOCKERS ниже (SMS gateway, deployment, monitoring, complaint model)

> Детальный changelog всех 50+ коммитов — в [`MASTERRYADOM_AI_CONTEXT.md`](MASTERRYADOM_AI_CONTEXT.md) **раздел 15**. История выполненного в этом файле — внизу, в виде краткого хронологического индекса.

- [x] **🔴 Category unification (pre-launch blocker resolved)** — closes core product-flow bug where studio-created services were invisible in the public catalog (catalog filters by `globalCategoryId`; new STUDIO-SERVICES-A UI was writing only legacy `ServiceCategory.categoryId`). **Strategy: forward-only unification, NO schema migration** — `Service.categoryId` was already optional in Prisma; only Zod validator enforced it. Changes: (1) `createStudioServiceSchema.categoryId` relaxed to optional + new optional `globalCategoryId` + new `proposerUserId` param for own-pending validation; (2) `createStudioService` lib helper accepts no-`categoryId` path + validates `globalCategoryId` against APPROVED-or-own-pending (mirrors master `listAvailableGlobalCategories`); (3) `/api/studio/services` POST passes `user.id` as `proposerUserId`; (4) `services-data.service.ts` rewritten — groups by `globalCategoryId` (not legacy `categoryId`), sidebar union'es APPROVED + own-pending + actually-used + synthetic «Без категории» bucket; (5) `add-service-dialog` rewritten — picker shows APPROVED + own-pending with `· на модерации` suffix; inline «+ Новая категория» calls `POST /api/categories/propose` (mirrors master service modal pattern), auto-selects freshly-proposed; (6) `service-detail-panel` PATCH body switches `categoryId` → `globalCategoryId`; (7) `add-category-dialog` (sidebar trigger) rewired from legacy `/api/studio/categories` → `/api/categories/propose`; (8) sidebar shows PENDING badge с tooltip «видна только вам». **CORE invariant verified: услуга студии → `globalCategoryId` → catalog filter** (`catalog.service.ts:332` `globalCategoryId: { in: categoryIds }`). Legacy `ServiceCategory` schema model untouched + endpoint `/api/studio/categories` untouched — still works для legacy `studio-settings-page.tsx` services tab. No data migration: pre-launch existing services без `globalCategoryId` show в «Без категории» bucket для admin, остаются invisible в catalog (user-acceptable per scope). New invariant #23: GlobalCategory APPROVED = public/global visibility; PENDING = creator scope only (CATEGORY-UNIFICATION-A)
- [x] **Studio gap fixes (chat icon + break management)** — closing two carryovers from STUDIO-BOOKINGS-A + STUDIO-SCHEDULE-A. **Chat icon (Option C — removed):** audit revealed `resolveChatAccess` allows only `clientUserId` OR `masterProvider.ownerUserId` — studio admin (third party) returns 403 by design. Variants A (route) and B (drawer) both require auth model change (chat is 1:1 client↔master, not 3-party). Removed broken icon + column from `booking-row.tsx` + `bookings-table.tsx`. UI_TEXT `bookingsV2.actions.openChat` left as dead string (Phase 7 cleanup). **Break management dialog:** new `ManageBreaksDialog` in `schedule/components/dialogs/` triggered from schedule header «Перерывы» button. Shows existing breaks for current day with delete + form to add new TimeBlock (BREAK type) for one-time master break. Uses existing `POST /api/studio/blocks` + `DELETE /api/studio/blocks/[id]`. Recurring weekly breaks → backlog (require `ScheduleBreak` model + `ScheduleEngine.editor.ts` integration). **Cache invalidation fix:** added `invalidateSlotsForMaster(masterId)` calls to `createStudioBlock`/`updateStudioBlock`/`deleteStudioBlock` in `src/lib/studio/calendar.service.ts` — pre-existing gap (endpoint созданный для legacy editor never invalidated public booking slot cache; new admin UI now triggers proper invalidation). Admin direct (no approval — инвариант #22). UI_TEXT `studioCabinet.scheduleV2.breakDialog.*` (~13 keys) + 4 new error keys (STUDIO-GAPS-FIX-A)
- [x] **Booking policy enforcement (BACKLOG gap closed)** — pre-launch backlog item «Booking flow enforcement» closed. The 3 booking-rules fields on `Provider` (`minBookingHoursAhead`, `maxBookingDaysAhead`, `acceptNewClients`) were settable in cabinets but **never validated at booking time**, and `visibleSlotDays` was settable but ignored by the public slots endpoint. **Audit findings:** `createBooking` core didn't reference any policy field; `/api/public/providers/[id]/slots` returned everything regardless of horizon or minimum-hours-ahead; direct API callers could bypass the slot UI entirely. **Closed via:** (1) new pure helper `src/lib/bookings/policy-enforcement.ts` exposing `earliestBookableUtc` / `latestBookableUtc` / `isWithinBookableWindow` / `assertBookingWindow` (throws `AppError("BOOKING_TOO_SOON" / "BOOKING_TOO_FAR", 400)`) / `assertAcceptsNewClient` (throws `AppError("NEW_CLIENTS_CLOSED", 403)`) / `clampVisibleSlotsHorizon` (single source of truth for the rules). (2) `resolveBookingCore` extended to select the 3 policy fields + 6-line block calling `assertBookingWindow` + conditional `assertAcceptsNewClient` (counts prior non-cancelled bookings only when `acceptNewClients=false` to skip the query on the common path). (3) `/api/public/providers/[id]/slots` extended to select `minBookingHoursAhead` + `visibleSlotDays` + clamp the requested `to` key via `clampVisibleSlotsHorizon` + drop slots before `earliestBookableUtc` from `baseSlots`. **Defense in depth:** slot UI never advertises an unbookable window AND `resolveBookingCore` (called inside `createBooking`) guards against direct API callers bypassing it. **3 new ErrorCodes** added: `BOOKING_TOO_SOON`, `BOOKING_TOO_FAR`, `NEW_CLIENTS_CLOSED`. **18 unit tests** in `policy-enforcement.test.ts` (boundary conditions, both sides of the window, the "no priors" gate, horizon clamp edges). **createBooking core untouched** — guards live in `resolveBookingCore` which is called by both `createBooking` and `createClientBooking`, so both paths inherit enforcement. **No schema migration.** Tests 247→265 (+18). Validation: typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅. Lint: my files clean; baseline drift 823/122→858/134 traced to a newly added reference file `.claude/references/PublicHeader.js` (not my code). **Full booking widget redesign deferred** — audit revealed multiple critical blockers (auth gate on `/api/bookings` POST, master-URL→profile redirect breaks scenario B, no studio slot aggregation, 600+LOC existing `StudioBookingFlow` is the working conversion path) that make a single-commit widget rewrite high-risk for the core conversion surface. Per spec's own rule «Лучше меньше но не сломать booking creation» — this commit ships the enforcement gap closure (high-value, low-risk) and defers the UX redesign to a dedicated sub-workstream below (BOOKING-WIDGET-A)
- [x] **Chat attachments backend (CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION) — schema + 7 steps, mirrors booking-reference** — user approved migration after the CHAT-FOUNDATION-A STOP-question. **Schema migration shipped, backend wired end-to-end, ZERO UI changes** (attachment picker/preview lives in CHAT-UI-A). **Audit findings:** booking-reference pattern fully understood — `validateReferenceAsset` in `src/lib/bookings/booking-extras.ts:60-89` is the canonical template (kind check → ownership check → entityType+entityId pending check). `uploadBookingReferenceAsset` in `src/lib/media/service.ts:360` is the upload helper that creates a PENDING MediaAsset with `entityId=pending:<userId>`. `Booking.referencePhotoAssetId` is set at booking-create time after the validator runs. **Schema migration (3 enum/column changes, all backwards-compatible):** (1) `MediaEntityType.CHAT_MESSAGE` added (new enum value) — covers `entityType` tagging for the asset; (2) `MediaKind.CHAT_ATTACHMENT` added (new enum value) — covers `kind` tagging; (3) `ChatMessage.attachmentMediaAssetId: String?` added (nullable FK to MediaAsset) + relation `attachmentMediaAsset` named `ChatMessageAttachment` with `onDelete: SetNull` — preserves the message body even if the asset is removed (mirrors `Booking.referencePhotoAsset` semantics); (4) `@@index([attachmentMediaAssetId])` for orphan-cleanup queries; (5) back-relation `MediaAsset.chatMessageAttachments ChatMessage[] @relation("ChatMessageAttachment")` added for completeness. Migration SQL hand-authored at `prisma/schema/migrations/20260519120000_add_chat_attachment/migration.sql` (the project's standard manual-migration approach — Postgres `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` × 2, `ALTER TABLE` for the nullable column, `ADD CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY` with `ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE`, `CREATE INDEX`). All existing `ChatMessage` rows get `attachmentMediaAssetId = NULL` → unchanged behaviour. **`npx prisma validate` ✅, `npx prisma generate` ✅** — schema accepted and client regenerated. **Backend (mirrors booking-reference verbatim, only differences = entityType/kind tags + per-user rate-limit key namespace):** (a) **`uploadChatAttachmentAsset`** added in `src/lib/media/service.ts` — byte-for-byte clone of `uploadBookingReferenceAsset` with `entityType=CHAT_MESSAGE` + `kind=CHAT_ATTACHMENT` + `entityId=pending:<userId>` + PENDING-then-READY status flow + storage put + rollback on failure. (b) **`POST /api/chat/upload-attachment`** added — byte-for-byte clone of `/api/bookings/upload-reference/route.ts` with the upload helper swapped + a chat-attachment rate-limit key (`30/hr` per user, slightly looser than booking-reference's `10/hr` since chat usage frequency is higher). Same Zod body schema (File ≤ 5 MB), same MIME sniffing via `file-type`, same Sharp re-encoding (PNG→WebP, JPEG retained, others→WebP). Returns `{ assetId }`. (c) **`src/lib/chat/attachment.ts`** added — `validateChatAttachmentAsset({ assetId, senderUserId })` mirrors `validateReferenceAsset` (asset exists / not soft-deleted / kind=CHAT_ATTACHMENT / `createdByUserId === sender.id` / `entityType=CHAT_MESSAGE` + `entityId` starts with `pending:`). `markAttachmentUsed({ assetId, messageId })` re-points entityId from `pending:<userId>` to `chat-message:<msgId>` — one-shot enforcement (same asset can't be reattached). Error codes use the existing `MEDIA_ASSET_NOT_FOUND` / `MEDIA_INVALID_KIND` / `MEDIA_INVALID_ENTITY` / `FORBIDDEN` from `src/lib/api/errors.ts`. (d) **`sendConversationMessage`** in `src/lib/chat/message-sender.ts` extended — accepts optional `attachmentMediaAssetId` in input; relaxes body-required validation to `body || attachment` (attachment-only messages are valid — natural chat UX); validates with `validateChatAttachmentAsset` before `prisma.chatMessage.create`; persists `attachmentMediaAssetId` in the create; calls `markAttachmentUsed` after create. Return shape gains `attachmentMediaAssetId: string | null` on the message. (e) **Both POST endpoints** — `/api/bookings/[id]/chat/messages` (legacy booking-scoped) AND `/api/chat/threads/[slug]/messages` (universal-chat) — Zod body schema relaxed: `body?: string trim max 1000` + optional `attachmentMediaAssetId?: string | null`, with a refine ensuring at least one of body/attachment is provided. Booking-scoped endpoint duplicates the validator+markUsed pattern inline (its message-creation logic doesn't flow through `sendConversationMessage`); universal endpoint passes through to the sender. (f) **Read-side selects** extended in 2 places: `messageSelect` constant in `src/app/api/bookings/[id]/chat/route.ts:14` adds `attachmentMediaAssetId: true`; `messages.select` in `src/lib/chat/conversation-aggregator.ts:367` adds `attachmentMediaAssetId: true`. The thread-preview select (`conversation-aggregator.ts:152`, returns only the latest message body+createdAt+senderType for the conversation list) intentionally does NOT include the attachment id — the conversation-list UI only needs the preview text. Conversation aggregator's read-mark `updateMany` is untouched (it operates on `chatId + readAt + senderType` filters, attachment-agnostic). **`resolveChatAccess` NOT touched** — studio admin denial preserved by-design (privacy invariant for РФ/152-ФЗ). Chat access + status tests still pass (274/274). **SSE / read receipts NOT regressed** — `deliverNotification` call in `sendConversationMessage` is untouched (same `CHAT_MESSAGE_RECEIVED` payload shape — attachment id is on the message row, not on the SSE event, so subscribers re-fetch the thread and see the attachment via the extended read-side select). The CHAT-FOUNDATION-A audit verified the SSE pipeline; this commit doesn't regress it. **Booking-reference upload NOT modified** — mirrored, not refactored (per spec). **UI NOT touched** — old chat surfaces work; attachment picker / preview / lightbox lives in CHAT-UI-A. Validation: typecheck ✅, lint **1 error / 3 warnings** preserved, encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **274/274 tests** ✅, **`npm run build` ✅**. **Pre-launch note:** the migration must be applied to production via `npx prisma migrate deploy` during the Phase 6 deployment window — it's safe (3 ADD operations, no destructive changes, all backwards-compatible). **Next:** CHAT-UI-A redesign over the now-complete backend (real-time SSE + receipts + attachments all wired) (CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION)
- [x] **Chat foundation audit (CHAT-FOUNDATION-A) — SSE + receipts already shipped, attachments → STOP for user decision** — audit-first commit per the BOOKING-WIDGET-FOUNDATION-A pattern («audit revealed blockers are softer than expected»). For chat the lesson applied even harder: of the 3 capabilities the spec scoped (real-time SSE, read receipts, attachments backend), **2 are already production-grade in the codebase** and the 3rd requires a schema migration that needs explicit user approval before this commit can ship code. **Audit findings:** (1) **SSE real-time — FULLY WIRED.** Full path verified: `src/lib/chat/message-sender.ts:189` calls `deliverNotification({ type: CHAT_MESSAGE_RECEIVED, payloadJson: { conversationSlug, bookingId, messageId, senderType, senderName, bodyPreview } })` after every new message. `src/lib/notifications/delivery.ts` invokes `publishNotifications()` which fans out via the Redis pub/sub notifier. `src/app/api/notifications/stream/route.ts:123` subscribes the SSE response stream via `notifier.subscribe(user.id, ...)`. Client hooks `src/features/chat/hooks/use-conversation-thread.ts:85` + `use-conversations.ts:64` both subscribe via `subscribeNotificationEvent(...)` and refetch on `CHAT_MESSAGE_RECEIVED` events — with `conversationSlug` payload check to scope updates to the open thread. **Both message sending API routes flow through `message-sender.ts`:** booking-scoped `/api/bookings/[id]/chat/messages` and universal `/api/chat/threads/[slug]/messages` (verified via grep). **Outcome: zero changes needed for real-time.** (2) **Read receipts — FULLY WIRED.** `ChatMessage.readAt: DateTime?` field exists in `prisma/schema/booking.prisma:178`. Two endpoints exist: `POST /api/bookings/[id]/chat/read` (booking-scoped legacy path) and `POST /api/chat/threads/[slug]/read` (universal-chat path). Both use `updateMany` with `chatId + readAt: null + senderType: { not: access.senderType }` to mark all unread incoming messages read in one query. The client `markRead` is wired in `use-conversation-thread.ts:97`. Backend semantics: "delivered" = `createdAt` set; "read" = `readAt` set. **Outcome: zero changes needed for receipts backend.** UI ticks (✓✓) are CHAT-UI territory. (3) **Attachments — REQUIRES SCHEMA MIGRATION → STOP per spec.** `ChatMessage` schema (lines 165-201) has no media/attachment field. `MediaKind` enum has `AVATAR / PORTFOLIO / MODEL_APPLICATION_PHOTO / CLIENT_CARD_PHOTO / BOOKING_REFERENCE` but no `CHAT_ATTACHMENT`. To enable attachments we'd need (a) a new enum value `MediaKind.CHAT_ATTACHMENT`, (b) a new optional column on `ChatMessage` like `attachmentMediaAssetId: String? + relation`, (c) corresponding index, (d) updated POST `/chat/messages` endpoint to accept attachment payload, (e) updated `sendConversationMessage` core to persist the link, (f) updated chat-read query to include attachment in selects, (g) sender-validation that the asset is owned by the sender (`createdByUserId === sender.id`) and unused. The spec is explicit: **«Schema migration ТОЛЬКО с явным подтверждением user — STOP, описать, спросить»**. Surface as the user-decision question below. **resolveChatAccess NOT touched** — studio admin denial preserved by-design (privacy invariant, especially relevant for РФ/152-ФЗ pre-launch). Verified: chat access + status tests still pass (274/274). **NO code changes ship in this commit** — the audit IS the deliverable, mirroring how BOOKING-WIDGET-FOUNDATION-A revealed the auth gate was actually elsewhere than expected. **Validation (no changes — confirming baseline preserved):** typecheck ✅, lint **1 error / 3 warnings** (unchanged from PHASE7-CLEANUP-A baseline), encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **274/274 tests** ✅. **Goal for CHAT-UI (stage 2):** all the backend the redesign needs (real-time fanout, receipts state) is already there — CHAT-UI can focus 100% on the visual redesign per `masterChat.png` + the two-panel layout + system-message cards + quick-reply chips + receipt ticks + (if attachments approved by user) attachment picker/preview. **Backlog spawned:** (a) **Chat attachments schema migration decision (user-blocker)** — requires explicit approval; describes the 7-step migration above. (b) **Typing indicators** — ephemeral, would need a separate transient SSE event (no DB persistence). Not a CHAT-UI dependency unless prioritised. (c) **Studio admin chat access** — by-design denial; revisit only if business need overrides privacy stance (CHAT-FOUNDATION-A)

(b) **Billing — `features.ts`/`mrr.ts`/`mrr-snapshot.ts`/`trial.ts` already covered** (29 + 6 + 6 + ~20 tests existing). **Pure helpers uncovered:** `utils.ts` (addMonthsUtc, sha256, formatTimeBucketUtc), `marketing-pricing.ts` (calcSavingsPercent, findPrice, listIncludedFeatures), `guards.ts` (3 AppError factories). (c) **Bookings — `reminders.ts`/`link-guest-bookings.ts`/`policy-enforcement.ts` already covered.** Pure uncovered: `flow.ts` (normalizeBookingStatus, resolveBookingRuntimeStatus, minutesUntilStart, ensureBookingActionWindow, ensureCancellationDeadline, canCancelOrReschedule — 6 pure functions for booking-state machine), `idempotency.ts` (buildCreateBookingIdempotencyKey + TTL constant). **Strategy: pure-function focus only.** Complex Prisma-mock paths (`createBooking` core, `cancelBooking`, `marketing-pricing.load`, `idempotency` Prisma-touching functions, `get-current-plan`) skipped per spec rule «лучше ядро покрыто чисто, чем всё поверхностно». **NEW FILES (5, ~84 unit tests):** (1) **`src/lib/billing/utils.test.ts` (15 tests)** — sha256 determinism + distinctness, formatTimeBucketUtc zero-padding + UTC purity, **addMonthsUtc with billing-critical edge cases**: positive delta preserving day, **day-clamping when target month is shorter** (Jan 31 + 1mo → Feb 28, NOT Mar 3 — critical for monthly billing periods), leap-year boundary, year rollover, 12-month yearly renewal, 6-month half-yearly, millisecond preservation, zero-month identity. (2) **`src/lib/billing/marketing-pricing.test.ts` (19 tests)** — `calcSavingsPercent`: null when monthly missing, null when periodMonths=1, null when not actually saving, 20% saving for project's flagship annual discount, 10% saving for 3-month bundle, integer rounding. `findPrice`: null when plan null, match by period, null for unknown period. `listIncludedFeatures`: empty when nothing enabled, excludes false booleans, includes enabled booleans, excludes zero/null limits, includes positive limits (both BOTH-scope on MASTER plan + STUDIO-only on STUDIO plan), filters out opposite-scope features, respects `limit` parameter, returns title alongside key, orders by `uiOrder`. (3) **`src/lib/billing/guards.test.ts` (6 tests)** — `createFeatureGateError` (403 + FEATURE_GATE code, feature+requiredPlan in details, optional requiredPlan), `createSystemDisabledError` (403 + SYSTEM_FEATURE_DISABLED), `createLimitReachedError` (409 + LIMIT_REACHED + max/current preserved). (4) **`src/lib/bookings/flow.test.ts` (32 tests)** — `normalizeBookingStatus` exhaustive 11-row mapping table (NEW→PENDING, PREPAID→CONFIRMED, STARTED→IN_PROGRESS, CANCELLED/NO_SHOW→REJECTED, others identity). `resolveBookingRuntimeStatus`: REJECTED unchanged regardless of time, FINISHED unchanged, future start = normalized, between start+grace = IN_PROGRESS, after grace = FINISHED, null startAtUtc falls back to normalized, zero-duration still fires IN_PROGRESS via grace window, BOOKING_FINISH_GRACE_MINUTES constant = 60. `minutesUntilStart`: null when missing, positive future, negative past, floors fractional. `ensureBookingActionWindow` (project's 60-min rule): BOOKING_TIME_REQUIRED on null, CONFLICT 409 when <60min, CONFLICT in the past, passes at exactly 60min boundary (inclusive), passes when comfortable. `ensureCancellationDeadline`: no-op when null/undefined, BOOKING_TIME_REQUIRED with deadline+null start, CANCELLATION_DEADLINE_PASSED 423 when deadlineHours ≤ 0, throws when past window, passes when comfortable, passes at exact deadline boundary (inclusive). `canCancelOrReschedule`: allows PENDING/NEW + CONFIRMED/PREPAID, blocks all terminal/in-progress, blocks CHANGE_REQUESTED. (5) **`src/lib/bookings/idempotency-key.test.ts` (6 tests)** — `buildCreateBookingIdempotencyKey` Redis-style namespacing, determinism, distinct keys per user/request, **guest-namespace path** (`guest:<phone>` from BOOKING-WIDGET-FOUNDATION-A), TTL constant = 600s. **One discovery during writing (NOT a bug, current code is correct):** `listIncludedFeatures` filters out features whose `appliesTo` doesn't match the plan scope, so a numeric limit like `maxTeamMasters` (STUDIO-only) is invisible on a MASTER plan — test was originally written assuming all-scope visibility, corrected to match actual semantics + added matching STUDIO-scope test for completeness. **No baseline-breaking bugs surfaced.** **Validation:** typecheck ✅, lint **1 error / 3 warnings** preserved (PHASE7-CLEANUP-A baseline — no new issues), encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **358/358 tests** ✅ (274 → 358, +84 new), **`npm run build` ✅**, **git diff shows only 5 new test files** (working code zero-changed — manual QA isolation preserved). **NOT covered (backlogged):** `createBooking` happy/conflict/idempotency integration (deep Prisma mock chain — better as integration test than unit), `cancelBooking` (same), `marketing-pricing.load` SSR path (requires Prisma + cache mocks), `idempotency.ts` Prisma-touching functions (`resolveBookingIdempotency` / `storeBookingIdempotency`), `get-current-plan.getCurrentPlan` (Prisma + cache + system flags chain), `deletion/*`, `visual-search/*`. **Backlog spawned:** booking-core integration tests with full Prisma mock or test DB (createBooking happy/conflict/idempotency/guest); cancelBooking integration test; marketing-pricing SSR load + cache invalidation integration test; idempotency Redis-locking integration test; deletion lib tests (account-wide cascade); visual-search openai+pgvector integration test; E2E tests (Playwright/Cypress — own large task) (TEST-COVERAGE-A)
- [x] **🎉 Chat UI redesign — final big platform commit (CHAT-UI-A)** — **ПЛАТФОРМА ДОСТРОЕНА**. Closes the chat UX consumption of CHAT-FOUNDATION-A (SSE + receipts) + CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION (attachments backend). **Audit reality-check applied per FOOTER/NAVBAR lesson:** spec scoped 5 redesign goals (two-panel layout, read receipts UI, attachment picker, system booking cards, quick-reply chips), but audit revealed **4 of 5 already production-grade** — only the attachment picker/render was the real gap. (a) **Two-panel layout** — `src/features/chat/chat-shell.tsx` (129 LOC) already renders conversation-list pane + thread pane with mobile-adaptive list↔thread navigation; no rewrite needed. (b) **Read receipts ✓✓** — `chat-window/bubble-meta.tsx` already renders `<Check>` (sent) → `<CheckCheck class="text-primary">` (read) via `isRead={Boolean(message.readAt)}`; backend already wired in CHAT-FOUNDATION-A. (c) **System booking cards** — `chat-window/system-message.tsx` already renders the booking snapshot card (service / date / duration / address / price / «Открыть запись» CTA) for SYSTEM-typed messages. (d) **Quick replies chips** — `composer/quick-replies.tsx` already shows quick-reply pills above the composer with per-session hide preference (localStorage key `chat.quickReplies.hidden`). (e) **Attachment picker — actual gap.** No file input, no preview, no upload integration; this is what the commit delivers. **Changes (4 files):** (1) **Types extended for attachment surface:** `src/lib/chat/thread-grouping.ts` adds `attachmentMediaAssetId: string | null` to `ThreadMessage` type + propagates the field through `injectDaySeparators`; `src/lib/chat/conversation-aggregator.ts` maps `attachmentMediaAssetId: message.attachmentMediaAssetId ?? null` in the flat-message read (Prisma select was already added in CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION); `src/features/chat/types.ts` extends `ThreadMessageDto` with `attachmentMediaAssetId: string | null` + JSDoc explaining why the renderer fetches via `/api/media/file/{id}` (same-origin cookie-auth path; `next/image` would lose auth context). (2) **`chat-window/message-bubble.tsx` rewritten** to render images above the text bubble when an attachment is present. Plain `<img>` (cookie-auth route — comment explains why not `next/image`) wrapped in `<a target="_blank">` for full-size view. Adaptive corner styling: when attachment is on top, the image takes the top corners and the text bubble takes the bottom corners (preserves speech-bubble tail visual); when there's only an attachment (no body), bottom corners follow `isMine` orientation. Lazy-load (`loading="lazy"`), max-height 320px, `object-cover`. `BubbleMeta` still rendered below with `isRead` from `readAt`. (3) **`composer/composer.tsx` rewritten** with attachment picker. New state machine `AttachmentState = idle | uploading | ready | error` discriminated union. Paperclip icon button (`lucide-react Paperclip`, `h-4 w-4`, disabled while sending/uploading/!canSend) triggers a hidden `<input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp">`. On file pick: (a) MIME validation against `ALLOWED_ATTACHMENT_MIME` set (`image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp`) → set error if invalid; (b) size check (≤ 10 MB) → set error if too large; (c) `URL.createObjectURL(file)` for preview, set `uploading` state; (d) POST `/api/chat/upload-attachment` with `FormData` containing the file (mirrors `booking-reference` upload pattern); (e) on success → `ready` with `assetId`; on failure → `error` with friendly Russian message + revoke preview URL. Preview tile (12×12 px image + label «Фото» or «Загружаем фото…» + X remove button) renders above the input row. Submit button disabled until `canSend && !sending && !isAttachmentUploading && (text.length > 0 || hasReadyAttachment)` — text-only OR attachment-only OR text+attachment are all valid (matches CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION `body || attachment` semantic). On submit: extends body with `attachmentMediaAssetId: readyAssetId` alongside `body: trimmed`; on success clears draft + revokes preview URL + resets to `idle`. **Object URL lifecycle correctly handled** — revoked on conversation change, on remove click, on success submit, on unmount cleanup. (4) **UI_TEXT extended:** `composer.attachAria` («Прикрепить фото»), `attachUploading`, `attachUploadFailed`, `attachInvalidType`, `attachTooLarge`, `attachRemoveAria`. **Existing `composer.footer` deliberately preserved** — current copy «ENTER — отправить · SHIFT+ENTER — новая строка · личные сообщения видны только вам и собеседнику» honestly describes message visibility («видны только вам и собеседнику») WITHOUT claiming end-to-end encryption. **Per spec strict reality-check rule «„все сообщения шифруются" — НЕ показывать без реального шифрования (152-ФЗ + false security promise)»**: messages are stored as plaintext in Postgres; adding «шифруются» would be false advertising + serious legal risk under 152-ФЗ. The existing footer phrase is honest and stays. **Aspirational elements per FOOTER/NAVBAR lesson — NOT fabricated, backlogged:** real online/presence status (no presence system → backlog), «N визитов» per-conversation client visit count (no CRM-visit aggregation in chat → backlog), typing indicators (no transient SSE event → backlog from CHAT-FOUNDATION-A audit), studio admin chat participation (`resolveChatAccess` denies third party by-design — privacy invariant for РФ/152-ФЗ → backlog, not fixed here). **Existing window header** uses `hasOpenBooking` as a proxy for «active conversation» with a green dot — preserved verbatim. **NO backend changes** — CHAT-FOUNDATION-A (SSE) + CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION (attachments) provided everything; UI consumes the contract. **`resolveChatAccess` NOT touched** — studio admin denial preserved by-design. **NO schema migration** — schema already migrated in CHAT-FOUNDATION-A-MIGRATION. **NO booking widget / auth / footer / navbar / sharedUI primitive touched.** Mobile-adaptive: list↔thread navigation already preserved in chat-shell (column-stack on mobile, two-pane on lg+); attachment preview tile + composer adapt naturally with flexbox. Both themes: image bubble uses `border-border-subtle bg-bg-input/40`; preview tile uses `border-border-subtle bg-bg-input/60`; text colors via tokens. Validation: typecheck ✅, lint **1 error / 3 warnings** preserved (same PHASE7-CLEANUP-A baseline — no new issues), encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **274/274 tests** ✅, **`npm run build` ✅** (production build green; `/cabinet/messages` + `/cabinet/master/messages` + chat shell with new composer all compile). **🎉 ПЛАТФОРМА ДОСТРОЕНА — это финальный большой коммит редизайн-спринта.** Cabinet Master (полностью) + Cabinet Studio (19 коммитов) + Cabinet Client (полностью) + Admin Panel (Phase 2 closed) + public master profile + public studio profile + booking widget (foundation + UI) + chat (foundation + migration + UI) + modal unification + catalog enhancements + footer + navbar — все production-ready. **Next step:** full QA pass across 4 surfaces (master cabinet / studio cabinet / client cabinet / public surfaces & widgets) before Phase 6 hardening (SMS gateway, multi-recipient notif, CI tests, catalog availability snapshot, aggregateStudioSlots SSR wiring, presence system, studio admin chat decision). **Backlog spawned:** (a) Real online/presence status (would need separate transient SSE pub/sub channel + UI dot logic), (b) Per-conversation client visit count chip (CRM `groupBookings` aggregation pulled into chat thread header), (c) Typing indicators (ephemeral SSE event), (d) Studio admin chat access (auth model decision — privacy concern), (e) End-to-end encryption (if business decides to deliver on the claim, significant infra work — currently footer wisely doesn't promise it), (f) Image lightbox UI (current: `<a target="_blank">` opens raw `/api/media/file/{id}` — works but not polished), (g) Multiple attachments per message (currently 1:1), (h) Voice messages / file types beyond images (CHAT-UI-A)
- [x] **FormDialog v2 — `submitVariant: "secondary"` + 2 unblocked migrations** — narrow extension of `FormDialog` to unlock dialogs whose submit-button visual intentionally uses the secondary tone. **Audit findings:** (a) `FormDialog`'s prior `submitVariant?: "primary" | "danger"` union explicitly excluded `"secondary"` even though `sharedUI Button` already supports it (`type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger" | "icon" | "wrapper"`). (b) Only **`pause-master-dialog`** had a true secondary-variant submit: `variant={mode === "pause" ? "secondary" : "primary"}` — the pause action is non-destructive but non-positive, so the design intentionally de-emphasises it. (c) **`invite-master-dialog` was MISCLASSIFIED in FORMDIALOG-MIGRATION-A.** That commit's BACKLOG entry grouped it with pause-master as «non-standard variant button», but on re-audit `invite-master-dialog.tsx:136` actually uses `variant="primary"` — it fits FormDialog V1 cleanly and was wrongly kept on ModalSurface. This commit corrects the misclassification. (d) **Grep-scan of all 35 still-kept dialogs** for hidden secondary-submit patterns returned only `pause-master` — no other kept dialog secretly needs `submitVariant="secondary"`. **The remaining 33 kept dialogs are structurally outside `submitVariant` scope** (multi-step, file-upload, OAuth, picker, retype-confirm, search-with-results) and require either primitive extension (which would touch ModalSurface — out of scope per spec) or a different pattern altogether. **Changes:** (1) `src/components/ui/form-dialog.tsx` — `submitVariant` union widened from `"primary" | "danger"` to `"primary" | "danger" | "secondary"`. Body of the submit `<Button variant={...}>` simplified from a `submitVariant === "danger" ? "danger" : "primary"` ternary to `submitVariant ?? "primary"` (passes through directly — `sharedUI Button` validates the union itself, default `"primary"` keeps V1 behaviour byte-identical). Doc comment added explaining the V2 rationale. **12 existing V1 callers untouched** (none passed `submitVariant`, all default to `"primary"` → identical render). **`ModalSurface` NOT touched** — 79 consumers untouched (per spec rule «79 — священны»). (2) `pause-master-dialog.tsx` migrated — `submitVariant={mode === "pause" ? "secondary" : "primary"}` preserves the original visual; `handleSubmit` body byte-identical; `handleClose` blocks while submitting (existing behaviour retained). (3) `invite-master-dialog.tsx` migrated — default primary submit; 3 input fields (phone/name/tagline) preserved with `disabled={submitting}` still wired; all validation (`normalizeRussianPhone`, name/tagline required checks) byte-identical; `reset()` helper kept and called on success. **Business logic byte-identical for both migrations.** **NO ModalSurface changes / NO sharedUI Button changes / NO new structural support in FormDialog (still no multi-step, no custom-footer — those remain ModalSurface territory).** **Realistic outcome:** 2 dialogs unblocked (FormDialog migration total now **14 of 49** — 12 V1 + 2 V2). The remaining 33 kept dialogs are structurally out of scope. Validation: typecheck ✅, lint **1 error / 3 warnings** preserved (same baseline as PHASE7-CLEANUP-A), encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **274/274 tests** ✅, **`npm run build` ✅**. **For QA:** pause-master + invite-master now share the unified submit-button styling/spinner/cancel-disable-while-submitting behaviour with the other 12 migrated dialogs. **Backlog:** Multi-step modal primitive (when worth unifying — currently 6-8 dialogs use bespoke multi-step), file-upload / OAuth dialogs (structurally outside FormDialog — acceptable to remain on ModalSurface). No further `submitVariant` candidates exist in the remaining kept set (verified via grep). Useful side-effect: FORMDIALOG-MIGRATION-A's `invite-master-dialog` misclassification corrected in this commit's report (FORMDIALOG-V2-A)
- [x] **FormDialog migration (12 of ~49 form-dialogs, conservative phased)** — first wave of MODAL-UNIFY-EXPLORE's `<FormDialog>` adoption per spec «лучше N чисто мигрированных + M честно оставленных». Migrated the **clearly-simple subset** (single form + Cancel/Submit + loading/error) where the FormDialog API maps 1:1; deferred multi-step / picker / custom-footer / file-upload / OAuth-flow dialogs to follow-up commits. **Audit found 49 dialogs using ModalSurface** (not 60 as estimated). Classification: ~12-15 clearly-simple, ~10 multi-section / multi-step, ~10 picker / non-form, ~10 file-upload / OAuth / complex flow, plus 2 with non-standard variant buttons (e.g. pause-master uses `variant="secondary"` for pause vs `variant="primary"` for activate — FormDialog only supports primary/danger so they stay on ModalSurface). **Migrated (12 files across 3 phases):** Phase 1 — studio cabinet (5): `cancel-booking-dialog`, `reject-dialog` (schedule-requests), `approve-dialog`, `delete-service-dialog`, `delete-package-dialog`, `report-review-dialog`. Phase 2 — admin cabinet (4): `approve-review-dialog`, `delete-review-dialog`, `cancel-subscription-dialog`, `refund-payment-dialog`. Phase 3 — general (3): `report-review-modal` (master), `edit-review-modal` (client), and `cancel-booking-dialog` was already counted. Each migration: imports swapped (`ModalSurface` → `FormDialog`), manual footer `<div className="flex justify-end gap-2">…</div>` block removed, manual submitting state removed (FormDialog manages internally — replaces `useState submitting` + `setSubmitting` boilerplate), manual error display block removed (FormDialog renders via `error` prop). **Business logic verbatim:** every `onSubmit` function body (fetch / API / mutations / success+error handling) preserved exactly — only the shell changed. Where `submitting` state was used in the body to disable form fields (e.g. `disabled={submitting}` on Textarea), the local state was kept; where it only drove the footer, it was removed. **Kept on ModalSurface (deliberate) with reasons:** `pause-master-dialog` + `invite-master-dialog` (non-standard variant prop), `assign-master-dialog` (picker — not a form), `manage-breaks-dialog` + `create-booking-dialog` + `move-booking-dialog` (multi-step / sub-form within), `add-service-dialog` + `add-category-dialog` (inline propose-category flow with extra button), `delete-studio-dialog` (retype-confirm with custom validation gating submit), `manual-booking-modal` (multi-section booking creation), `service-modal` + `bundle-modal` + `package-modal` (multi-section forms with sub-rows + dynamic add/remove), `break-modal` + `exception-modal` (multi-day picker + type cards), `upload-modal` + `crop-modal` + `edit-item-modal` (portfolio file-upload + image crop UI), `email-verify-modal` + `telegram-connect-modal` (OAuth/OTP flows with timer + special submit), `visual-search-modal` + `portfolio-preview-modal` (search-with-results + image preview, not form), `login-required-modal` (auth gate, not form), `duplicate-groups-modal` (admin cities list view), `merge-city-dialog` + `create-city-dialog` + `plan-edit-dialog` + `plan-change-dialog` + `create-category-dialog` + `reject-confirm-dialog` + `client-reschedule-modal` + `client-review-modal` + `propose-time-modal` + `create-offer-modal` + `edit-offer-modal` + `reject-application-modal` + `reschedule-modal` (master + cabinet) — all left because the spec emphasizes "лучше M честно оставленных, чем N сломанных насильно" before a major QA pass; future micro-sweep can pick them up «при касании». **Lint surprise:** removing the local `submitting` state in 3 admin dialogs (cancel-subscription, refund-payment, delete-review) caused the existing `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule to fire on the `useEffect(() => { setReason(""); }, [open])` form-reset blocks — pre-existing pattern, was previously paired with `setSubmitting(false)` which masked the rule's trigger. Resolved with localized `// eslint-disable-next-line` comments + intent comment (same approach `email-verify-modal` already uses for its identical pattern). Lint baseline preserved at **1 error / 3 warnings** (unchanged from PHASE7-CLEANUP-A baseline — no new issues introduced). **NO business-logic changes / NO schema migration / NO primitive rewrites (ModalSurface/FormDialog/Drawer/ConfirmModal untouched) / NO ConfirmModal+useConfirm changes / NO Chat / NO booking widget / NO auth / NO footer / NO navbar touched.** Phased self-validation: typecheck after Phase 1 ✅, typecheck after Phase 2 ✅, typecheck after Phase 3 ✅, final lint ✅, **274/274 tests** ✅, encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, **`npm run build` ✅**. **For QA:** the migrated dialogs now share a consistent submit-button label / spinner / animation / cancel-disable-while-submitting behavior — easier to verify one pattern instead of 12 hand-rolled variants. The remaining 37 dialogs continue to work exactly as before on ModalSurface — visually slightly different (their footer alignment + spinner labels are bespoke) but functionally identical. **Backlog:** Phase 4 micro-sweep — pick up the ~10 «kept» dialogs that are close-to-simple but have one quirk (custom variant button, inline propose-category, retype-confirm) when there's time. Multi-step / picker / file-upload / OAuth dialogs are not FormDialog candidates without API extension (which would touch the primitive — out of scope). FormDialog v2 with `submitVariant: "secondary"` support would unlock 2-3 more migrations; not urgent (FORMDIALOG-MIGRATION-A)

- [x] **Catalog `slotPrecision` + `availableToday` (карточка)** — точечная интеграция 2 полей в catalog card. **Audit findings:** (1) `Provider.slotPrecision` is a `String` field (NOT enum) with values `"exact"`/`"today_free"`/`"date_only"` (canonical type lives in `src/lib/schedule/editor-shared.ts:86`). (2) The catalog listing query (`searchCatalogProviders` in `src/lib/catalog/catalog.service.ts`) **does not compute per-card `nextSlot`** — the listing hardcodes `nextSlot: null` and exposes only the cheap `availableToday: boolean` snapshot already maintained on `Provider`. (3) **The card never actually rendered availability today** — its `nextSlot` field was always null on the listing path, so the «зелёная точка» chip was always hidden. (4) `visibleSlotDays` is NOT relevant to the card today (the card has no horizon/calendar — it shows a single chip), and would only matter if a precomputed `nextSlot` snapshot existed (cross-ref BOOKING-WIDGET-A which clamps it on the slots endpoint). **Changes (no schema migration, no schedule-engine pass on the listing):** (1) New pure helper `src/features/catalog/lib/slot-precision-format.ts` — `normalizeSlotPrecision` + `formatAvailability({precision, nextSlotStartAt?, availableToday?, timeZone, fallbackToOpen?})` returns `{ label, tone }`. Switch: `exact + nextSlotStartAt` → «Ближайшее: {when}»; `today_free + availableToday` → «Сегодня свободно»; `date_only + nextSlotStartAt` → «Свободно {date}»; soft fallback when `exact` is set but no precomputed slot → `availableToday` still gets «Сегодня свободно» (graceful per scope rule «не вводить N× schedule engine»). Optional `fallbackToOpen` returns «Запись открыта» (neutral tone) when no positive signal exists; the card uses `fallbackToOpen: false` to keep its silent-when-nothing-to-say UX. (2) `searchCatalogProviders` Provider `select` extended with `slotPrecision: true` (already had `availableToday: true`) — both cheap scalar columns on the same row, no JOIN, no engine. `CatalogProviderItem` DTO extended with optional `slotPrecision` + `availableToday` fields; existing `todaySlotsCount: 1` signal preserved for backwards compat. (3) `catalog-card.tsx` replaces the inline `slotText` computation with `formatAvailability(...)`; chip rendering now branches on `availability.tone` — `"available"` keeps the emerald dot + emerald text, `"neutral"` shows muted tone. Card visual frame (22a/22b) unchanged — only the availability chip's text logic + tone branch. (4) `UI_TEXT.catalog2.card.availability` subtree (~4 keys). **`visibleSlotDays` consciously not consumed here** — surfaced as backlog because the card has no horizon UI, and the only place it could matter (precomputed `nextSlot` snapshot) doesn't exist yet. **Perf invariant preserved:** the catalog listing remains `N × O(1)` lookups (no schedule-engine fan-out). Validation: typecheck ✅, lint baseline 858/134 preserved (external `.claude/references/PublicHeader.js` drift carried over), encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, 274/274 tests (no test surface changed). **Backlog spawned:** catalog availability pre-computation — when a snapshot pipeline lands (provider's nearest free slot computed off the booking write path, cached on Provider or in Redis), `exact`/`date_only` providers will render their preferred precise availability and `visibleSlotDays` will clamp the lookahead horizon (consistent with BOOKING-WIDGET-A's slots endpoint clamp). Today the cost of computing it on the listing is prohibitive (N × `buildSlotsForDay`). Honest tracking: «Public catalog filtering — visibleSlotDays card surface» remains medium priority; `slotPrecision` is now consumed but its `exact` / `date_only` paths only fire when the snapshot exists (CATALOG-ENHANCEMENTS-A)
- [x] **Modal unification (Variant 1: «достроить existing»)** — `ModalSurface` extended backwards-compatibly + new `FormDialog` wrapper + new unified `<Drawer>` primitive, then 5 independently-rolled drawers migrated to the new shell. **Background:** MODAL-UNIFY-EXPLORE discovery showed 79 files / 251 imports already consume `ModalSurface` (canonical) — but 5 drawers each rolled their own portal + scroll-lock + escape + animation, and ~60 form-dialogs duplicated `mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2 + submitting state + error display` boilerplate. **Changes (3 primitives):** (1) **`ModalSurface` extended backwards-compat** — new optional props: `size: "sm"|"md"|"lg"|"xl"|"full"` (default `lg` = original `max-w-2xl`), `header: { title, subtitle?, icon? }` (rich header with auto `aria-labelledby`; plain `title` string still works), `footer?: ReactNode` (rendered below children in the `mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2` slot). framer-motion `AnimatePresence` wraps overlay (fade 180ms) and panel (fade + scale 0.97→1 + slight y-translate, 220ms cubic-bezier `[0.22, 1, 0.36, 1]`). **All 79 existing callers work unchanged** — old API (`{open, onClose, title, className?, children}`) renders identically. (2) **NEW `FormDialog`** (`src/components/ui/form-dialog.tsx`) — wraps `ModalSurface`, built-in `<form>` element, footer (Cancel + Submit) with internal `submitting` state, internal `try/catch` error display, externally-controllable `error`/`submitDisabled` props, `submitVariant: "primary"|"danger"`. For new dialogs going forward — legacy 60 not rewritten (migration «при касании»). The gate is at `/api/bookings` POST (hard `getSessionUser(req)` + `requireRole([CLIENT])`). The widget calls `createBooking` client-fn which posts to that endpoint and shows an auth modal on `AUTH_REQUIRED`. (2) **Scenario B partially wired** — `?master=<id>` is already parsed in `page.tsx` (lines 118-119) and passed through to StudioBookingFlow as `initialMasterId` when the route is a STUDIO provider. Master-direct URL `/u/anna/booking` redirects away — solo masters use the booking UI embedded in `PublicMasterProfilePage` instead. (3) **Slot aggregation is already client-side** — `StudioBookingFlow` does `Promise.all(masters.map(fetchMasterAvailability))` + has `ANY_MASTER_ID` UI flow. The missing piece was a **server-side** aggregator for SSR'd panels. **Changes:** (1) **NEW pure helper** `src/lib/schedule/studio-slot-aggregation.ts` (~150 LOC) — `aggregateStudioSlots()` + pure `mergeByStart()` (unit-testable). Reuses `listAvailabilitySlotsPaginated` per-master (no engine rewrite). Filters MasterService rows by invariant #24 (ACTIVE masters only — `ownerUserId IS NOT NULL && isPublished`). Returns `AggregatedStudioSlot[]` carrying `availableMasterIds` + deterministic `earliestMasterId` (input order). Safety cap `maxMasters` (default 12) to bound N×Prisma. (2) **9 unit tests** `studio-slot-aggregation.test.ts` — pure merge logic (empty, single-master, dual-master same-time, order preservation, sparse 3-master matrix, chronological sort, dedup, unknown-master ordering). (3) **Guest booking enabled (surgical adaptation, NOT rewrite):** `createBooking` + `resolveBookingCore` + `resolveBookingExtras` + `idempotency.loadBookingForIdempotency` all accept `clientUserId: string | null`. When null: ownership-self check skipped, `acceptNewClients=false` gate still applies (guest = new client with 0 priors → blocked, same UX as a logged-in new client), hot-slot anti-fraud skipped (no userId for cancellation history), reference-photo attach rejected with FORBIDDEN (uploads are auth-only). Idempotency + rate-limit keys namespaced by `clientUserId ?? guest:${clientPhone}` — guest retries dedup safely on the same phone. Booking row saved with `clientUserId: null` (column already nullable in schema; `link-guest-bookings.ts` post-signup linker handles attachment when the same phone signs up later). **`createBooking` itself NOT rewritten** — idempotency / conflict / transaction / rate-limit / UTC plumbing preserved verbatim. (4) **`/api/bookings` POST adapted** — uses `getSessionUserFromRequest(req)` (nullable). Session user → require CLIENT role (preserves original semantics: MASTER/STUDIO can't book through this endpoint). No session → guest path, `clientUserId = null`. Legacy `slotLabel`-only path still requires a session (createClientBooking adaptation deferred — booking widget always sends startAtUtc/endAtUtc, so this branch isn't reached). `invalidateRecentMastersCache` guarded behind effectiveClientUserId. (5) **Widget UI minimal adaptation** — `booking-flow.tsx` collects `guestName` + `guestPhone` inputs in a new card under the booking summary when no session. Submit no longer triggers the auth modal on `AUTH_REQUIRED` (path removed); falls through to the standard error display. Existing auth modal markup left in tree (showAuthModal stays false) — Phase 7 cleanup. (6) **Scenario detection confirmed working** for studio + `?master=` (already wired before this commit; no code change needed). Solo master booking (`/u/anna/booking` URL) deliberately not enabled — solo masters use the embedded widget on their profile, redirect preserves the existing UX. **NO schema migration. NO createBooking core rewrite. NO schedule engine rewrite.** Enforcement (policy-enforcement.ts) preserved — guest is treated as a new client, so `acceptNewClients=false` providers block them with the same NEW_CLIENTS_CLOSED 403 as a logged-in new client. `link-guest-bookings.ts` post-signup linker preserved + tests still pass. - [x] **Seed consolidation (4 showcase phones + P2002 fix)** — pre-launch hardening of the test-data seed pipeline. **P2002 root cause:** `seed-showcase-master.ts:120` used `upsert({ where: { email } })` then set `phone: PHONE` in the `create` branch. Any drift between seed generations (different canonical phone or stale row holding the canonical email/publicUsername) made the `create` path collide on the `phone` unique constraint. New helper `prisma/seeds/test-data/helpers/ensure-user.ts` exports `ensureUserByPhone(input)` — phone-first upsert with a Phase 1 "shadow release": any row that holds the canonical email OR publicUsername with a *different* phone gets its unique fields renamed to per-row placeholders (`released-<id>@<SEED_EMAIL_DOMAIN>`, `publicUsername=null`) before the Phase 2 upsert-by-phone proceeds. Result: rerunning `npm run seed:test` without reset no longer throws P2002 even if canonical identifiers drift between commits. **Showcase phone schema 100/200/300/400:** four memorable demo phones outside the generic `+7900000xxxx` range — `+79991000000` solo master (Анна — was `+79991000009`, migrated by reset+rerun), `+79992000000` studio owner (Виктория Vision), `+79993000000` master-in-studio (Марина, ordinal 1 of Vision's 7 masters — overrides her ordinal-derived `+79992000001`), `+79994000000` platform admin (new). New constants in `helpers/markers.ts`: `SHOWCASE_PHONE_{MASTER,STUDIO_OWNER,STUDIO_MASTER,ADMIN}` + `SHOWCASE_PHONE_PREFIXES = ["+79991","+79992","+79993","+79994"]` (covers all showcase phones including Vision team). `reset.ts` extended to match these prefixes alongside the generic prefix + email domain — defense-in-depth even if a showcase row loses its email marker. New `seed-showcase-admin.ts` seeds an `[CLIENT, ADMIN]`-roled user with no domain data (just unlocks `/admin` routes for testing Phase 2 surfaces — SUPERADMIN intentionally not granted to keep blast radius small). Applied `ensureUserByPhone` in master showcase + studio showcase owner + each of 7 studio masters + admin seed. `seedEmail` extended to accept `"admin"` role. **Generic seed kept stable** — `seedProviders` / `seedClients` upsert-by-email pattern works fine because of unique slugs; risk-of-breakage outweighs marginal "more data" benefit; richness goal already met via STUDIO-SHOWCASE-SEED (7 masters / 35 services / 56 bookings / 15 reviews). **Idempotency:** `npm run seed:test` → `npm run seed:test` (no reset between) now succeeds cleanly. Validation: typecheck/lint(823/122)/encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, 247/247 tests. **Not executed against live DB** (Postgres unavailable in dev env) — verified structurally via Prisma client types (SEED-CONSOLIDATION-A)

## 🔴 PRE-LAUNCH BLOCKERS

### Безопасность

> **SECURITY-AUDIT-A (2026-05-23)** — read-only application-level audit, 8 categories + bonus. **Result: strong posture — 6 of 8 categories CLEAN.** Sprint security patterns (auth guards, #25/#26 privacy invariants, HMAC tokens, Zod validation, cross-tenant `ensureStudioRole`) held consistently. **3 findings:** 1 🟠 High (email OTP code-in-logs regression-gap), 1 🟡 Medium (JSON-LD `<`-escaping, CSP-mitigated in prod), 1 🔵 Low (Yandex Maps key domain-restriction, ops). Full per-category breakdown in AI_CONTEXT раздел 10 + раздел 15.

- ~~**🟠 SEC-1 Email OTP `code` logged on send-failure**~~ ✅ **CLOSED — OTP-LOG-DEV-GUARD-A (2026-05-23)**. Per user decision, applied a stricter+broader fix than originally scoped: **NODE_ENV guard pattern** across all 3 OTP log surfaces (phone + 2 email) — code visible in dev/staging logs (testing convenience + saves SMSC credits) but **never lands in production logs**. New shared `isProduction` flag in [`src/lib/env.ts`](src/lib/env.ts) (computed alongside `isVkAuthEnabled`/`isSmsConfigured`) + new shared [`src/lib/logging/masking.ts`](src/lib/logging/masking.ts) (`maskPhone`/`maskEmail`). 3 OTP routes use the pattern `...(isProduction ? {} : { code })` + mask phone/email in payloads. Phone OTP also gets `code` back conditionally (regaining dev convenience SMS-GATEWAY-A had stripped). 10 unit tests (`masking.test.ts`) pin: phone masking format, email masking format, conditional-spread shape (`code` present when `isProduction=false`, absent when `=true`). 562 → **572 tests** ✅. typecheck/lint(1-3 baseline)/build/check:ui-text ✅. Pattern documented in AI_CONTEXT раздел 13 as standard for secret-in-logs class.
- ~~**🟡 SEC-2 JSON-LD embeds user content without `<`-escaping**~~ ✅ **CLOSED — FAST-WINS-BATCH-A (2026-05-23).** New `safeJsonLd(schema)` helper in `src/lib/seo/schema.ts` escapes `<` → `<`. Applied to 4 sites (audit found 4): `layout.tsx`, `faq/page.tsx`, `u/[username]/page.tsx` ×2. 4 helper tests pin behavior. Defense-in-depth — CSP still primary defense. Below: historical entry.
- **🟡 SEC-2 JSON-LD embeds user content without `<`-escaping (historical, closed)** — [`src/app/(public)/u/[username]/page.tsx:387,406`](src/app/(public)/u/[username]/page.tsx#L387) (+ `faq/page.tsx`, `layout.tsx`) render `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(schema) }}` where `schema` embeds user-controlled fields (master/studio name, description, review text, author names). Raw `JSON.stringify` escapes `"` but NOT `<`/`/`, so a `</script>` breakout is structurally possible. **Mitigated in production** by the nonce + `strict-dynamic` + no-`unsafe-inline` CSP in `src/proxy.ts` (injected scripts/handlers won't execute) — so NOT exploitable for script execution in prod. Residual risk: HTML-structure/SEO breakage + dev has no CSP. **Fix:** add `.replace(/</g, "\\u003c")` to the JSON-LD `__html` output (standard hardening) — small shared helper across the ~3 JSON-LD sites. Defense-in-depth, schedule (not launch-blocking given CSP). Pattern: new-gap-not-yet-patterned.

No other module imports it; runtime behavior identical. **TC-2 — `validateChatAttachmentAsset` + MIME/size constants (10 tests)** at [`src/lib/chat/attachment.test.ts`](src/lib/chat/attachment.test.ts): 7 tests on the validator predicate (asset-not-found, soft-deleted, wrong-kind, ownership-mismatch=403, already-attached=409, wrong-entityType=409, happy-path) using existing `vi.hoisted` + `vi.mock("@/lib/prisma")` pattern from `chat-attachment-acl.test.ts`; 3 tests pin `MEDIA_ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES` (exactly 3 image formats Sharp re-encoder supports), forbidden formats explicitly excluded (PDF/SVG/HEIC/video/HTML — attack-surface minimization), `MEDIA_MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES` = 10 MiB. **gitignore restructure follow-up:** `!docs/QUALITY-GATES.md` exception added so cross-doc link from SPRINT-PATTERNS works for fresh-clone readers. - **🔴 #1 Email change unique-collision (historical, closed)**: [`src/app/api/cabinet/user/profile/email/request-verify/route.ts:72-75`](src/app/api/cabinet/user/profile/email/request-verify/route.ts#L72-L75) пишет `email` **сразу** в `UserProfile.email` (`@unique`) НА ЭТАПЕ ЗАПРОСА КОДА, до подтверждения. Если адрес занят (другой юзер, или released-`*` seed-row, или сам себе в другой записи) — Prisma бросает P2002 → handler возвращает 500. UX-провал: user видит 500 на бизнес-операцию. **Root cause**: схема не имеет `pendingEmail` placeholder — `email` колонка одновременно «текущий email» и «целевой email подтверждения». **Fix options** (отдельный промпт EMAIL-VERIFY-FIX): (a) добавить `pendingEmail String?` колонку (schema migration) — писать туда на request, переносить в `email` после verify; (b) **без migration**: catch P2002 и вернуть 409 `EMAIL_ALREADY_USED` с user-friendly сообщением + НЕ обновлять `email`/`emailVerifiedAt` до verify (использовать OtpCode как единственный носитель target-email, читать оттуда в verify-endpoint). Не правлено сейчас — задача после полного QA клиентского

## 🟡 MEDIUM PRIORITY

### FEED-PORTFOLIO-N1-FIX-A (spawned from PERFORMANCE-AUDIT-A 2026-05-23 — PERF-1) 🔴 launch-concern
- **What**: refactor 4 sites in [`src/lib/feed/portfolio.service.ts`](src/lib/feed/portfolio.service.ts) (`listPortfolioFeed`, `listHomePortfolioFeed`, `getPortfolioDetail`, `similarItems` loader) to avoid loading ALL `MasterService` rows per service.
- **Why**: hot path home feed. 50-item feed × ~2 services × hundreds of masters offering popular services = ~10K rows fetched for ~100 matching. Cache-MISS path + per-user authenticated path hit every request.
- **Approach**: (a) remove `masterServices` from nested service include; (b) collect `(masterProviderId, serviceId)` tuples from the loaded rows; (c) batch-fetch matching `MasterService` rows in one query (`WHERE (masterProviderId, serviceId) IN tuples`); (d) build lookup `Map<masterId-serviceId, MasterService>`; (e) `resolveServiceOption` consumes the map instead of inline filtering. Same query count (2 instead of 1 nested), but no over-fetch.
- **Scope**: ~half-day. 4 sites share the same `services.include.service.masterServices` pattern → can refactor once, reuse.
- **Risk**: medium — touching hot path; comprehensive testing needed. Mitigated by existing pagination/caching + existing `buildPortfolioSnapshot` test coverage.
- **Trigger**: schedule pre-launch — risk-reward favors fixing before traffic.

### PRISMA-INCLUDE-WHERE-CI-CHECK (structural prevention for PERF-1 class)
- **What**: `scripts/check-prisma-include-where.mjs` — AST walk over `src/` looking for `prisma.X.findMany({ include: { Y: { include: { Z: { select: {...}, ... } } } } })` patterns where inner `Z` has no `where` clause. Flag for manual review (false positives possible when over-fetch is intentional).
- **Why**: PERF-1 wasn't caught at PR time because no automation flagged it. Adding this script prevents new instances + can sweep existing code.
- **Scope**: ~half-day — AST parsing via `typescript` compiler API or `acorn` + Prisma pattern recognition. Add to `quality-gates.yml` after typecheck.
- **Output**: list of file:line + suggestion to add `where` or explicit `// over-fetch-ok: <reason>` annotation.

### MONEY-BRAND-TYPE-A (spawned from BUSINESS-LOGIC-AUDIT-A 2026-05-23)
- **What**: introduce `type Kopeks = number & { readonly __brand: "Kopeks" }` brand type + factory `kopeks(n: number): Kopeks` + propagate through ~20 sites that currently use bare `number` for money (`MarketingPlanPrice.priceKopeks`, `BillingPayment.amountKopeks`, `MrrInput.priceKopeks`, `Service.price` / `priceOverride`, `bookedServicePrice`, etc).
- **Why**: bare `number` means TypeScript can't catch «accidentally subtract rubles from kopeks» or «pass duration to a price field». Brand type forces explicit cast at boundaries (Prisma read → `kopeks(row.priceKopeks)`), compile-time guard everywhere else. Pattern proven in TypeScript ecosystem (e.g. `tsbrand`).
- **Scope**: ~half-day. (1) Define `Kopeks` brand + factory in `src/lib/types/money.ts`; (2) propagate through `marketing-pricing.ts` / `mrr.ts` / `mrr-snapshot.ts` / `booking-core.ts effectivePrice` / `createBooking.ts bookedServicePrice` / `webhook-processor.ts` (~20 sites); (3) `Service.price` Prisma type stays `Int` but read-time cast `kopeks(row.price)`. No schema migration.
- **Cost vs value**: ~half-day cost. Value: compile-time guard against entire bug class. No current bug — proactive.
- **Trigger**: schedule when next major billing/pricing surface change happens — opportunistic with that work.

### OTP-EMAIL-LOGIN-RACE (spawned from EMAIL-VERIFY-FIX-A 2026-05-23)
- **Что**: [`src/app/api/auth/otp/email/verify/route.ts:71-82`](src/app/api/auth/otp/email/verify/route.ts) does `findUnique({email})` then conditional `create({email})` — between read and create two parallel first-time logins for the same email could both miss `existingProfile`, both call `create`, one P2002s. Currently un-handled → 500 на one of the two parallel users.
- **Why deferred (different shape from EMAIL-VERIFY-FIX-A)**: this is a **registration first-time-login race**, NOT an «email already used by another account» scenario. The right fix is **recover-by-re-read** (mirror the [`conversation-slug.ts`](src/lib/chat/conversation-slug.ts) + [`detect-city.ts`](src/lib/cities/detect-city.ts) + [`mrr-snapshot.ts`](src/lib/billing/mrr-snapshot.ts) pattern): on P2002 → re-fetch by email, return the row created by the race winner. NOT a 409 to user (they did nothing wrong — both are legitimately trying to log in with their own email).
- **Probability**: very low (requires two parallel OTP-verify requests within ms with identical email and no pre-existing profile). Realistic only if a user double-taps verify button.
- **Scope**: wrap the `create` in try/catch, on P2002 → re-run `findUnique({email})`, return that row. ~10 LOC. Mirror existing P2002-recovery pattern verbatim.
- **Trigger**: low-priority; address when audit/QA surfaces real-user impact OR as part of next post-launch hardening sweep.

### SMS-MONITORING-A (spawned from SMS-GATEWAY-A 2026-05-23)
- **Что**: admin balance widget + daily cron alert на low-balance trigger (`SMS_LOW_BALANCE_THRESHOLD`, default 500 ₽ in env)
- **Зачем deferred**: ширина scope SMS-GATEWAY-A; admin balance UI требует admin-cabinet integration, daily cron — отдельный queue job + worker handler. Когда provider live в prod — monitoring критичен («забыли пополнить → SMS не уходят → users не могут login»)
- **Готовое infrastructure**: `getSmsProvider().checkBalance()` уже работает (используется SMSC.ru `/balance.php`), `SmsBalanceResult` typed
- **Scope**: (1) admin dashboard tile «SMS баланс» вызывающий `checkBalance()` + visual urgency below threshold; (2) `sms.balance.daily` queue job + worker handler + endpoint `/api/sms/balance/check/run` (mirrors MRR-CRON pattern); (3) external cron registration в production (Yandex Cloud Scheduler)
- **Trigger**: после YANDEX-DEPLOY-A + SMSC live в prod (Phase 6.2)

### SMS-PROVIDER-FALLBACK (spawned from SMS-GATEWAY-A 2026-05-23, 🔵 nice-to-have)
- **Что**: multi-provider failover (SMSC.ru → SmsAero как backup) когда primary outage
- **Зачем**: single-point-of-failure через 1 provider не критично для MVP, но рискованно при high-volume launch
- **Готовое infrastructure**: abstract `SmsProvider` interface уже swap-friendly, factory просто extended
- **Когда**: если случится первый incident с SMSC outage или metrics показывают >0.1% delivery failures

- **Per-team-member notification routing** — once preferences exist, admins should be able to route notification types to specific team members (e.g. only finance person gets BILLING events). Cross-ref STUDIO-NOTIFICATIONS-A backlog
- **Studio policy editor on this page** — current implementation is read-only with a deep link to the schedule editor (which is the only edit surface today). Add an inline editor that posts through a thin studio-policy mutation
- **Anonymisation on studio delete** — `deleteStudioCabinet` uses cascade `deleteMany` which removes rows rather than anonymising them. For 152-ФЗ / GDPR compliance, switch to anonymised soft-delete (replace personal fields, keep aggregate metrics). Known platform-wide backlog
- **Phase 7 cleanup: legacy `studio-settings-page.tsx`** — 837-LOC big component still serves portfolio/profile sub-routes. After portfolio + profile main get their own redesigns, this whole file can go away
- **Legacy sub-routes consolidation** — `/settings/{profile,portfolio,general,public,features}` all render the same big component with different `initialTab`. After portfolio + profile redesign, fold into the new 5-section page (or kill the sub-routes)

🔵 **Nice-to-have:**
- **Studio working-hours default editor** — surfaces `WeeklyScheduleConfig` for the studio provider as a default schedule. Today, per-master schedule is the only authoritative source
- **Custom studio policy text** — free-form rules (e.g. «дети с родителями», «без животных») surfaced on public profile. Needs `Studio.publicRules: string[]` schema column
- **Studio danger zone audit trail** — log archive/delete actions to `AdminAuditLog`-style table so support can reconstruct history if a studio disputes

Not in scope without explicit business signal — keep watching usage to see if real studios run into this

### Из STUDIO-REVIEWS-A (2026-05-17)

🟡 **Medium priority:**

- **Bulk reply** — "Ответить всем" CTA in header to auto-publish a templated thank-you to multiple unanswered reviews at once. Useful for studios with backlogs. Needs UI for template editing + per-master attribution check + cap (e.g. 50/operation)

Solution would be daily snapshot like `MrrSnapshot` — applies to dashboard occupancy too. Cross-ref STUDIO-DASHBOARD-A backlog
- **Per-master reply identity** — currently every reply is labelled «Ответ студии» regardless of who typed it (admin or master). If a future business case wants masters to sign their own replies, add `repliedByUserId` field + UI toggle. Not in scope right now per explicit spec
- **Reply edit on studio surface** — `editReviewReply` exists in service layer but the studio reviews UI only offers POST (first reply). PATCH would let the studio fix typos / refresh the public response
- **Top/anti-top masters by rating** — reference design showed this, replaced with "top services by reviews" per spec. If business wants the leaderboard back, derive from `Review.rating` group-by `masterId` aggregate

Phase 6: admin button «Запустить geocoding» → re-run `detectCityFromAddress` на всех `cityId IS NULL` providers, throttled аналогично существующему backfill script

🟡 **Medium priority:**
- **`City.tag` поле для кастомных кодов** — сейчас hardcoded map топ-30 RU/KZ городов + fallback `slug.replace(/-/g,"").slice(0,3).toUpperCase()`. Если admin захочет кастомный код для регионального города (например, «Сочи» → `SCH` уже захардкожено, но для других — нет UI override). Миграция + UI field
- **`@deprecated` legacy AdminCities** — `src/features/admin/components/admin-cities.tsx` после ADMIN-CITIES-UI. Phase 7 cleanup sweep. Также `UI_TEXT.admin.cities.*` (~80 keys)
- **Duplicate detection cache** — сейчас `findDuplicateGroups()` пересчитывается на каждый list-request (O(n²) для geo pass). При <100 городах OK; при росте — Redis cache TTL 60s, invalidate при CUD operations
- **Selected city URL state + filter conflict** — если admin выбрал city, потом сменил status filter и выбранный город не подходит под фильтр, `?selected=` остаётся в URL. Detail panel показывает stale city. Сейчас CitiesFilters сбрасывает `?selected=` при смене tab, но не при изменении search

🔵 **Nice-to-have:**
- **Drag & drop sortOrder** в admin/cities — сейчас Input number в edit form, неудобно для переупорядочивания топ-10 cities
- **Bulk action: hide/show selected** — checkbox column + bulk toolbar для массового скрытия городов одной операцией
- **Region grouping в UI** — после добавления City.region группировать таблицу по региону (collapse/expand)
- **City import from CSV** — для масс-загрузки городов (с lat/lng/timezone columns)
- **Provider listing по городу** — drill-down в detail panel: показать топ-10 providers в выбранном городе с переходом в `/admin/users` filtered by city
- **Map preview в detail panel** — `next/image` с Yandex Static Maps API для preview координат
- **Levenshtein-based duplicate detection** — текущий algorithmic pass находит exact-normalize и geo-proximity дубли, но не «опечатки» типа «Кранодар»/«Краснодар». Levenshtein ≤ 2 без geo proximity

- **Block / Unblock account** — 🟡 **partial done**: schema migration выполнена в MIGRATIONS-PRELAUNCH-A (`blockedAt`/`blockedByUserId`/`blockedReason` + self-FK + index). **Остаётся:** UI flow (More menu в admin users list) + endpoints `POST /api/admin/users/[id]/block` + `/unblock` + behaviour gate в auth middleware (login refusal для blocked users) + mutation rejection в `requireAuth()`. ### Из MRR-SNAPSHOTS-A audit (2026-05-13)

🔴 **Pre-launch blockers:**
- **External cron setup для MRR snapshot** — endpoint `/api/billing/mrr/snapshot/run` готов + worker handler работает, но **без cron schedule snapshots не создаются автоматически**. Yandex Cloud Scheduler / GitHub Actions / любой внешний cron должен быть настроен **до launch** (см. `docs/runbooks/mrr-snapshot-cron.md`). Без этого admin никогда не увидит реальную MRR дельту — будет вечный «—»

🟠 **High priority:**
- **MRR breakdown by tier/scope** в snapshot — поле `breakdownJson` зарезервировано в схеме, заполнение в отдельном коммите когда понадобится drill-down («MRR by Premium Master» и т.п.)
- **MRR snapshot monitoring** — alert если snapshot за сегодня не создан до 12:00 UTC (cron силент failure signal). Через `MONITORING_TELEGRAM_*` env vars

🟡 **Medium priority:**
- **Backfill historical MRR** — для дат до запуска cron snapshots отсутствуют → admin увидит «—» в первые 30 дней после deployment. Возможен исторический backfill через `UserSubscription.startedAt` + audit log замеры, но сложно и неточно. Решение по необходимости
- **MRR snapshot retention** — 1 row/day = 365 rows/year. Acceptable. Но при добавлении hourly или per-tier snapshots — нужна retention policy (drop > 2 years)
- **Снапшоты race-condition stress test** — current implementation handles P2002 fallback, но edge-case под 100+ concurrent crons не покрыт

🔵 **Nice-to-have:**
- **Hourly snapshots** для real-time MRR tracking (если бизнес потребует)
- **MRR forecast** — линейная/экспоненциальная экстраполяция из historical snapshots
- **Snapshot diff UI** — admin может сравнить любые две даты в admin/billing
- **Cohort tracking** через snapshots (когорта мастеров, зарегистрировавшихся в данный месяц, через 3/6/12 мес)
- **Worker schedule helper** — internal helper to enqueue daily-recurring jobs (сейчас external cron; future могло бы быть внутреннее)

### Из REVIEW-SOFT-DELETE-A audit (2026-05-14)

🟡 **Medium priority:**
- **Permanent delete cron / retention** — soft-deleted reviews накапливаются вечно. Перед массовым launch нужна retention policy: после N дней (GDPR требует обычно 30-90 дней) `deletedAt < now-N days` → real DROP. Backlog: queue job `review.cleanup.permanent-delete` + worker handler. Аналогично `media.cleanup`
- **Re-leave review после admin removal** — если admin удалил отзыв клиента, клиент сейчас не может оставить новый на тот же booking (unique constraint на `bookingId`). UX-вопрос: либо разрешить re-leave (требует disambiguation бы между active/deleted в unique constraint), либо явно блокировать с сообщением «Отзыв был удалён администратором, повторно оставить нельзя». Сейчас silent block
- **Restore review UI flow** — отложено user-решением. Если когда-то понадобится: tab «Удалённые» в admin/reviews + restore action + `REVIEW_RESTORED` AdminAuditLog (enum value уже зарезервирован в MIGRATIONS-PRELAUNCH-A) + restore notification (новый `NotificationType`)
- **Display marker «Удалён администратором»** в author's cabinet — UX option вместо silent hiding. User видит свой отзыв с пометкой что админ его убрал + reason если есть
- **`Review.deletedByUserId` UI surfacing** — admin сейчас не видит **кто** удалил review (admin vs author vs legacy admin) в queryable way. AdminAuditLog имеет actor, но joining требует UI work. Backlog: добавить in `listAdminReviews` если соответствующий tab появится

🔵 **Nice-to-have:**
- **Bulk admin restore** — массовая операция для emergency rollback (например, если плохой админ удалил много отзывов)
- **Soft-delete history per target** — admin может посмотреть «все отзывы удалённые для master X» при разбирательстве жалоб
- **Author-friendly «My deleted reviews» tab** — отдельно от active reviews, transparency option
- **Notification «Ваш отзыв восстановлен»** — если когда-то добавим restore UI flow

- **Phase 7 sweep #2** — после Cabinet Studio sprint накопится новый legacy: текущий `src/features/cabinet/master/schedule/master-schedule-editor.tsx` (1 488 LOC `@deprecated`, оставлен потому что зависит от studio cabinet redesign) + всё что появится после studio refactor

### Из ADMIN-REVIEWS-A audit (2026-05-13)

🔴 **Pre-launch blockers:**
- ~~**`Review.deletedAt` миграция (soft delete)**~~ ✅ выполнено в REVIEW-SOFT-DELETE-A (2026-05-14). Hard delete заменён на soft в admin + user paths, 18 query sites фильтруют `deletedAt: null`, ratings recalc игнорирует deleted, idempotent re-delete. Restoration = manual SQL (`UPDATE Review SET deletedAt = NULL`)

🟠 **High priority:**
- **`ReviewReport` модель для multi-reporter** — сейчас Review.reportedAt/reportReason/reportComment один-к-одному (последний reporter перезаписывает). При production-нагрузке множественные жалобы на один review должны храниться отдельно с user_id reporter'а, чтобы admin видел breakdown reasons + count + smart deduplication. Schema migration + миграция existing данных + API + UI redesign (column "Жалобы (N)" вместо single reason)
- ~~**`NotificationType.REVIEW_DELETED_BY_ADMIN`**~~ ✅ выполнено в NOTIFICATION-TYPES-A (2026-05-14). Enum + dispatch с автоматическим резолвом targetName (master.name или studio.provider.name) + reason. Body — `buildReviewDeletedByAdminBody`
- **`NotificationType.REVIEW_REPORT_RESOLVED`** — миграция enum + рассылка автору-репортёру при admin approve («Ваша жалоба рассмотрена, нарушений не найдено»). Закрывает loop репортёр ↔ модерация
- ~~**AdminAuditLog таблица**~~ ✅ выполнено в ADMIN-AUDIT-INTEGRATION (2026-05-13). Все 16 admin endpoints (billing/users/cities/catalog/reviews/settings) пишут в `AdminAuditLog` через shared helper. `BillingAuditLog` остаётся forever-parallel для billing — accepted decision

Если потребуется (например, удаление persondata из отзыва вместо полного delete), нужна UGC policy: admin edit только masking sensitive data, audit log обязателен, original_text snapshot. Phase 6 после legal review
- **Advanced filters** — by date range, by reportReason value, by target type (master vs studio), by author. Сейчас 3 tab + search достаточно для MVP, при росте reviews нужно расширение
- **Urgency tuning** — `isUrgentReport` сейчас true для rating=1 OR reason=OFFENSIVE. При тюнинге по результатам real moderation: добавить rules (например, INAPPROPRIATE + ≥ 5 reports → critical, новый author + 1 review → low priority). Configuration через AppSetting key
- **Восстановление удалённых** — после `Review.deletedAt` migration: UI tab «Удалённые» + restore action. Currently impossible (hard delete)
- **Bulk approve / bulk delete** — checkbox column + bulk action toolbar. Сейчас по одной (admin spam-pattern fight requires bulk)
- **Inline reply от admin** — admin может оставить публичный ответ на review от имени System («Платформа: отзыв проверен модерацией, информация подтверждена»). Schema поддерживает `Review.replyText` сейчас занят master/studio ответом — нужно отдельное поле `systemReplyText` или JSON breakdown

🔵 **Nice-to-have:**
- **Deleted tab** — после `Review.deletedAt` migration: третий+ tab «Удалённые за период» с restore action
- **Drill-down на target/author** — клик на «Анна Соколова» → переход к `/admin/users?q=...` filtered; клик на «Анна (мастер)» → переход к `/u/<username>` или admin detail когда появится
