No `window.confirm`/`window.prompt` calls — uses `ModalSurface` throughout + `CancelBookingDialog`/`MoveBookingDialog` siblings. **The problems MASTER-BOOKING-UI-FIX-A fixed don't exist in studio bookings** because the action surface is fundamentally different. 12th audit-zero-code case across both waves. (2) **#3б root cause confirmed**: «Новая запись» header button ([bookings-header.tsx:44](src/features/studio-cabinet/bookings/components/bookings-header.tsx)) opens `CreateBookingDialog` with `startAtUtc={null}`. The form has **no time picker** — only renders the read-only «time card» when `startAtUtc !== null`. User fills name/phone/service/master → click submit → `!startAtUtc` branch returns generic error → user confused. Calendar empty-slot click works because it prefills `startAtUtc`. (3) **#3в normalizeRussianPhone existing but submit-only**: `normalizeRussianPhone` ([src/lib/phone/russia.ts:10](src/lib/phone/russia.ts)) is the canonical validator (used in auth/OTP flows). Submit path at line 101 calls it correctly. But **no real-time feedback** — user types invalid phone, clicks submit, sees error only then. (4) **Other form fields smoke check**: clientName has `clientNameRequired` check on submit — same pattern (no real-time but at least it errors). Email is not in this form (no validation gap). Service select uses dropdown (no free-text). No serious bonus gaps. **Fixes:** (1) **#3а** — NO ACTION. Documented inheritance: audit-zero-code via fundamentally-different action surface. Studio inherits `BookingActionMenu` shared primitive — modifications to that primitive would be a cross-cabinet change, not in scope. (2) **#3б** — **Option (a) chosen**: time picker rendered as `<Input type="datetime-local">` when `startAtUtc === null` (header-button flow). Pre-fill flow (calendar click) keeps the existing read-only card. New helpers `utcIsoToLocalInput` / `localInputToUtcIso` handle the conversion both ways. New local state `startAtLocal`. Submit reads `startAtUtc ?? localInputToUtcIso(startAtLocal)` — either path produces a valid ISO. New error key `startAtRequired` for explicit «Укажите дату и время записи» (replaces silent generic). (3) **#3в** — real-time phone validation: `phoneIsValid = trimmed.length === 0 || normalizeRussianPhone(trimmed) !== null`. Empty input treated as «still typing» (neutral); invalid typed input gets red border (`border-red-500` + dark variant) + inline `<p>` with `clientPhoneInvalid` text + `aria-invalid={true}`. Submit button `disabled={!phoneIsValid}` + `title` tooltip with error message. **Reuse pure** — no new validator, no new regex; same helper everywhere in the project. **NO schema migration**. **`createBooking` backend untouched** — UI fix only (added time picker + real-time validation, both feed existing API). **flow.ts state-machine** untouched. **`BookingActionMenu` shared primitive untouched** — would be a cross-cabinet modification, out of scope. **MASTER-BOOKING-UI-FIX-A primitives (PromptModal/role-aware DTOs)** untouched — confirmed not needed for studio (different action surface). **STUDIO-APPROVE-400 + STUDIO-RESCHEDULE-VALIDATION + STUDIO-MASTERS-PRIVACY** not crossed (different surfaces). **Calendar click flow preserved** — read-only time card renders when pre-fill present. **Tests**: 11 new ([create-booking-validation.test.ts](src/features/studio-cabinet/bookings/create-booking-validation.test.ts)) covering phone-acceptance predicate (empty / canonical +7 / 8-prefix / incomplete / garbage / formatted with separators) + effective-start resolution (pre-fill / local picker / null / malformed / pre-fill-wins-over-local). 484 → **495 tests** ✅. UI_TEXT: 1 new key (`startAtRequired`). lint baseline 1/3 preserved. typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **Carryover**: 4 studio cabinet bugs remain (#5 client write / #6 cleanup / #7 services sort / #8 schedule settings large).

- **STUDIO-MASTERS-PRIVACY-FIX-A** — 3/8 commit studio cabinet QA-волны. **3rd application of HMAC opaque-URL pattern** (after CHAT-FOUNDATION attachment + MASTER-CLIENTS-FIX-A history-filter). **Audit findings:** (1) **2 URL leak sites** confirmed: [master-detail-header.tsx:105](src/features/studio-cabinet/masters/components/master-detail-header.tsx) («Расписание мастера» Link) + [master-detail-week-schedule.tsx:25](src/features/studio-cabinet/masters/components/master-detail-week-schedule.tsx) («В календарь» Link). Both used `?masterId=${detail.id}` — raw prisma cuid in URL. (2) **«Грубая ошибка» pattern repeats**: [calendar page handler](src/app/(cabinet)/cabinet/studio/calendar/page.tsx) accepted ONLY `view` + `date` params, **`masterId` query was IGNORED entirely** — link "took user to full studio calendar with no master highlight". Same dual bug pattern as STUDIO-APPROVE-400-FIX-A (privacy + functional). (3) **Existing HMAC infra**: [`client-key-token.ts`](src/lib/master/client-key-token.ts) from CLIENTS-FIX-A is the canonical pattern (purpose `client-history-filter`, 24h TTL, base64url body + HMAC-SHA256 sig, `AUTH_JWT_SECRET`, timingSafeEqual). [`chat-attachment-token.ts`](src/lib/media/chat-attachment-token.ts) is the first application (purpose `chat-attachment-read`, 15min TTL). **Direct mirror** for studio — no new infrastructure. (4) **No other linking sites** — grep confirmed `?masterId=cuid` only at the 2 named buttons. Notifications/email don't carry master deep-links. **Fixes:** (1) **NEW** [`src/lib/studio/master-view-token.ts`](src/lib/studio/master-view-token.ts) — `signStudioMasterViewToken({masterId, studioId})` + `verifyStudioMasterViewToken({token, studioId})`. Payload `{mid, sid, exp, purpose: "studio-master-view"}`. TTL 24h (mirrors CLIENTS-FIX-A). Studio-scoped via `sid` claim — cross-studio share-link rejected (privacy 152-ФЗ). Distinct purpose claim prevents cross-replay with chat-attachment + client-history tokens. (2) **`StudioMasterDetail.viewToken: string`** added to DTO ([types.ts](src/features/studio-cabinet/masters/server/types.ts)). Populated server-side in [master-detail.service.ts](src/features/studio-cabinet/masters/server/master-detail.service.ts) via `signStudioMasterViewToken({masterId: provider.id, studioId: input.studioId})` — uses canonical `provider.id` (not query input which may be publicUsername). (3) **2 Link sites updated**: «Расписание мастера» + «В календарь» both now use `?master=${encodeURIComponent(detail.viewToken)}`. Master id no longer in URL. (4) **`MasterDetailWeekSchedule` prop renamed** `masterId` → `viewToken` for clarity. Caller `master-detail-panel.tsx` passes `detail.viewToken`. (5) **Calendar page handler extended**: new `master?: string` searchParam, decoded via `verifyStudioMasterViewToken({token, studioId})`. Invalid/cross-studio/expired tokens silently fall through to `focusMasterId: undefined` (full calendar) — URL is shareable-within-cabinet, not a security boundary; failure modes don't disrupt UX. Threads `focusMasterId` through `<StudioSchedulePage>` to `<DayGrid>`. (6) **DayGrid auto-scroll**: `useRef + useEffect` (mount-gated) scrolls focused master's column into view with `behavior: "smooth", inline: "center"`. Focused column gets `ring-2 ring-primary/40 ring-inset` so the visual reason is obvious. Closes the functional gap (handler now actually does something with the param) alongside the privacy gap. **NO schema migration**. **HMAC infrastructure reused verbatim** — no new signing module, just sibling token-helper file in `src/lib/studio/`. **MASTER-CLIENTS-FIX-A + CHAT-FOUNDATION token helpers untouched** — pattern extension via repeated application, not modification. **STUDIO-APPROVE-400-FIX-A + STUDIO-RESCHEDULE-VALIDATION-A** not crossed (separate endpoints). **policy-enforcement.ts** untouched. **Master cabinet 91 regression + studio #1+#2 24 tests** all pass. **Tests**: 11 new ([master-view-token.test.ts](src/lib/studio/master-view-token.test.ts)) mirroring CLIENTS-FIX-A test pattern point-for-point: roundtrip (custom + realistic cuid); **URL privacy** (token bytes don't contain masterId OR studioId substring); cross-studio rejection; expired token rejected; tampered signature + tampered body rejected; malformed/empty token rejected; cross-purpose replay rejected (foreign `client-history-filter` purpose claim correctly rejected). 473 → **484 tests** ✅. lint baseline 1/3 preserved. typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **Carryover**: 5 studio cabinet bugs remain (#4 bookings / #5 client write / #6 cleanup / #7 services sort / #8 schedule settings).

- **STUDIO-RESCHEDULE-VALIDATION-A** — 2/8 commit studio cabinet QA-волны. **3 связанные validation gaps** в studio `moveStudioBooking` flow закрыты shared helpers. **Audit findings:** (1) **Existing `moveStudioBooking`** ([bookings.service.ts:126](src/lib/studio/bookings.service.ts)) checks target master ACTIVE (STUDIO-BUGS-FIX-A) и updates booking row + optionally duration/pricing — **никаких других guard'ов**. (2) **#1а Master ↔ Service**: `strategy === "CHANGE_SERVICE"` branch READS `MasterService` rows но только для duration/price overrides; `strategy === "KEEP_SERVICE"` (UI's default) **никогда не reads MasterService → no compatibility check**. Booking moves to incompatible master silently. (3) **#1б Conflicts**: `moveStudioBooking` **не вызывает `ensureNoConflicts`** — race window allows double-booking the same time slot. (4) **#1в Work hours**: **полностью отсутствует check** — user observed booking moved to 4 AM accepted; не показывается в schedule grid (вне отображаемого диапазона), но row сохранён. (5) **policy-enforcement.ts** ([src/lib/bookings/policy-enforcement.ts](src/lib/bookings/policy-enforcement.ts)) — established pattern для pure rule helpers from BOOKING-WIDGET-A; precedents `assertBookingWindow`/`assertAcceptsNewClient`. Add `assertMasterPerformsService` + `assertWithinMasterWorkHours` следуют тому же style. (6) **Work hours data source**: per-master `WeeklyScheduleConfig` + `WeeklyScheduleDay.template{startLocal,endLocal}` + per-date `ScheduleOverride` (already populated by STUDIO-APPROVE-400-FIX-A seed migration). Defaults Mon-Sat 10-19 (per Vision template) когда config отсутствует — matches showcase + master expectation. (7) **MoveBookingDialog UI** uses raw `<input type="datetime-local">` + master `<Select>` filtered только по `isAvailable` — нет service-compat gating. **Fixes:** (1) **policy-enforcement.ts extension** — 2 new pure helpers `assertMasterPerformsService({ hasEnabledMasterService })` (throws `MASTER_SERVICE_MISMATCH` 422) + `assertWithinMasterWorkHours({ bookingStartMinutes, bookingEndMinutes, window })` (throws `OUTSIDE_WORK_HOURS` 422) + new type `MasterWorkWindow`. **Pure** — no Prisma deps, fully unit-testable. (2) **2 new error codes** в `ERROR_CODES` literal union ([errors.ts](src/lib/api/errors.ts)): `MASTER_SERVICE_MISMATCH` + `OUTSIDE_WORK_HOURS`. (3) **DB-aware resolver** `resolveMasterWorkWindow(masterProviderId, dateUTC)` в [studio/bookings.service.ts](src/lib/studio/bookings.service.ts) — reads `ScheduleOverride` для date (precedence) + `WeeklyScheduleDay` + template hours; falls back to project defaults (Mon-Sat 10-19 / Sun off) if no config. Single helper, 2 parallel queries. (4) **`moveStudioBooking` enforcement** — 3 gates inserted после ACTIVE-master check и перед mutation transaction: (a) query enabled `MasterService` rows for target master + booking serviceIds, assert ALL match (covers multi-service bookings); (b) `resolveMasterWorkWindow` + `assertWithinMasterWorkHours` for new start; (c) inline `findMany` conflicts excluding self booking, `SLOT_CONFLICT` 409 if overlap (mirrors `ensureNoConflicts` pattern with self-exclusion for move semantics). (5) **UI side — `ScheduleMasterColumn` extended with `serviceIds: string[]`**. Populated in (a) `schedule-data.service.ts` day-view loader (extra Prisma `select` on `masterServices` filter `isEnabled: true`); (b) week-view loader (same); (c) `cabinet/studio/bookings/page.tsx:loadShellExtras` (reuses existing `masterServices` query, builds inverse `servicesByMaster` map). (6) **`MoveBookingDialog` master picker** — new prop `bookingServiceId`. Master `<option>` rendered with `disabled={!performsService}` + suffix «не выполняет эту услугу» in label; warning hint below select когда current `masterId` doesn't perform booking's service. **Visibility-over-hiding** per spec — option remains in list для context, just locked. Backend `assertMasterPerformsService` still validates server-side (defense-in-depth). (7) Caller `booking-action-menu.tsx` threads `booking.serviceId` through to dialog (field already on `ScheduleBookingCell` DTO). **NO schema migration**. **Reject endpoint untouched**. **MASTER-RESCHEDULE-FIX-A patterns reused** — policy-enforcement.ts extension consistent with master pattern, defense-in-depth pattern preserved. **STUDIO-APPROVE-400-FIX-A не пересечён** — separate flow (schedule request approve vs booking reschedule). **flow.ts state-machine** (32 tests) preserved. **`ensureNoConflicts` / `createBooking` / `approveChangeRequest` / `rejectChangeRequest`** untouched. **Master cabinet 91 regression tests + studio #1 13 tests** pass. **Tests**: 11 new ([reschedule-policy.test.ts](src/lib/bookings/reschedule-policy.test.ts)) covering: master-service mismatch throws 422 with correct code/message; work-hours boundary cases (start at open / end at close / 4 AM rejection / end after close / inactive day / corrupt minutes / partial overlap both sides); distinct error codes for two rules. 462 → **473 tests** ✅. UI_TEXT: 2 new keys (`masterIncompatibleSuffix` + `masterIncompatibleHint`). lint baseline 1/3 preserved. typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **Carryover**: 6 studio cabinet bugs remain (#3 privacy URL → reuse master HMAC, #4 bookings, #5 client write, #6 cleanup, #7 services sort, #8 schedule settings large).

- **STUDIO-APPROVE-400-FIX-A** — 1/8 (по критичности — функциональность сломана). Studio admin approve schedule request → 400 «Некорректное тело запроса.» в 2 UI entry-points (#2 dedicated schedule-requests page + #7 notifications inline approve). **Audit findings:** (1) **Reject endpoint работает корректно** ([reject/route.ts](src/app/api/studio/schedule/requests/[id]/reject/route.ts) — readsBody `{ comment }`, requires non-empty, updates status). **Approve endpoint** ([approve/route.ts](src/app/api/studio/schedule/requests/[id]/approve/route.ts)) — **не валидирует body вообще** (no zod schema, no body read). 400 идёт **изнутри** `applySchedulePayload`, не из endpoint! (2) **Точное место 400**: [`src/lib/schedule/unified.ts:546-552`](src/lib/schedule/unified.ts#L546-L552) — `validateSchedulePayload` throws `AppError("Некорректное тело запроса.", 400, "INVALID_BODY")` когда payload не имеет `templates[]` / `weekly.days[]` / `overrides[]` shape. (3) **Корень проблемы — seed data**: STUDIO-SHOWCASE-SEED создаёт fake `payloadJson` shapes `{ kind: "WEEKLY", delta: "shift +5 minutes Saturday" }` и `{ kind: "OVERRIDE", dateOffsetDays: 7, isDayOff: true }` для QA visualization. **Эти shapes не match НИ `SchedulePayload` (legacy), НИ `EDITOR_V1` (canonical)** — оба пути валидации falля. (4) **Audit-zero-code 10-й case** (как 9 из master-волны): backend correct, seed bug. Backend honestly rejects malformed payload. Но UX poor — studio admin не понимает что не так. **Fix strategy — Сценарий A + защитная обёртка:** (1) **Seed fix**: новый helper `buildVisionSchedulePayload(extraException?)` в [`seed-showcase-studio.ts`](prisma/seeds/test-data/seed-showcase-studio.ts) строит валидный `EDITOR_V1` payload с 7-day weekSchedule (Sun off, Mon-Sat 10:00-19:00 FLEXIBLE) + optional exception. Marina's request — no-op (re-apply current schedule); Elena's — adds day-off exception на дата +7 дней. Approve flow теперь applies real snapshot end-to-end. (2) **Approve endpoint defensive wrap**: try/catch around `applyScheduleSnapshot` / `applySchedulePayload`. Catches `AppError(status=400, code="INVALID_BODY")` и rewraps в `AppError(422, "INVALID_REQUEST_PAYLOAD", "Не удалось применить расписание: данные запроса повреждены или устарели. Попросите мастера отправить заявку заново.")` — actionable copy. Studio admin теперь понимает что делать (попросить мастера resend), вместо confusing "Некорректное тело запроса.". 422 status semantically точнее (data understood but unprocessable). Other 400 codes pass через без re-wrap. (3) **New error code** `INVALID_REQUEST_PAYLOAD` добавлен в `ERROR_CODES` union ([errors.ts](src/lib/api/errors.ts)). **NO schema migration.** **Reject endpoint untouched** — образец working pattern preserved. **NO master cabinet волны защиты ослаблены** — 91 master regression tests pass без изменений. **Both UI entry-points** (schedule-requests page + notifications inline approve) автоматически работают через одну backend починку — никаких UI changes. **Tests**: 13 new ([approve-payload-validation.test.ts](src/app/api/studio/schedule/requests/approve-payload-validation.test.ts)) — purely-functional coverage of (a) `isScheduleEditorRequestPayload` detector (accept/reject across 8 cases), (b) `normalizeScheduleEditorRequestPayload` happy path + legacy-shape rejection, (c) the rewrap predicate (`status === 400 && code === "INVALID_BODY"` → wrap; otherwise rethrow). 449 → **462 tests** ✅. lint baseline 1/3 preserved. typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **log-error 429** наблюдение от user — defensive rate limit на log endpoint **работает корректно** (UI много раз retry'ила failed approve → rate limited логирование), не bug, не trogать. **Production note**: existing rows в БД с placeholder seed payloads будут greet'ить 422 (clearer error) вместо 400 после deploy — но новый seed run заменит их валидными payloads.

### 2026-05-21 — Post-redesign QA support + аудиты

- **🎉 MASTER CABINET QA-ВОЛНЫ ЗАВЕРШЕНА (8/8)** — все 8 fixes shipped, 89 регрессионных тестов накоплены (358 → 449), 2 новых invariants добавлены (#25 master CRM privacy + #26 chat attachment ACL), 0 schema migrations. Per-commit summary:
  1. ✅ **MASTER-PRIVACY-FIX-A** (audit-zero-code) — privacy boundary master notes/tags **подтверждена** + 13 regression tests добавлены (type-level + source-level). Invariant #25 формализован.
  2. ✅ **CLIENT-SHOWCASE-SEED-A** — seed для QA (Елена Петрова `+79995000000`), 9 bookings разных статусов + 3 reviews + 5 notifications + ModelApplication. Аудит вскрыл 2 НЕ закрытых бага (🔴 email-verify 500 + 🟠 OAuth UI confusion).
  3. ✅ **MASTER-CHAT-ATTACHMENT-FIX-A** — 3 связанные chat-attachment проблемы (полоска / 403 / cuid в URL). Common root cause: `ensureCanReadMedia` не покрывал `CHAT_MESSAGE` entity. Opaque-token URL scheme + 19 tests. Invariant #26 формализован.
  4. ✅ **MASTER-BOOKING-UI-FIX-A** — 2 связанные booking-action UI: initiator-aware visibility (`actionRequiredBy`) + browser native dialogs → ModalSurface (PromptModal primitive). 11 new tests.
  5. ✅ **MASTER-RESCHEDULE-FIX-A** — 2 связанные reschedule (#5а pending-block + #5б free-slots) + bonus policy enforcement gap closed. 4 new tests.
  6. ✅ **MASTER-DASHBOARD-FIX-A** — 3 связанные dashboard «состояние во времени» (#1а past attention + #1б hydration audit-driven + #3 expired action buttons). Shared `action-state.ts` predicates. 13 new tests.
  7. ✅ **MASTER-CLIENTS-FIX-A** — 3 связанные «Клиенты» (#7а URL без cuid + handler fix + #7в auto-status tooltips + #6 notes feature realisation). HMAC token primitive. 11 new tests. Tags ручное → backlog.
  8. ✅ **MASTER-MODELS-FIX-A** — multi-applicant cascade (confirm endpoint cascades siblings → REJECTED, mirrors closeOfferWithCascade) + client UX softening + master CRM «откликался» marker. NO migration. 11 new tests.

  Process patterns established (репликабельные для следующих волн):
  - **Audit-driven scope decision** — 9 раз: каждый fix начинался с аудита кодa, 9 commits закрылись либо «zero-code» (только regression tests / docs) либо «backend ready, только UI gap» — экономит часы и предотвращает scope creep
  - **Visibility-over-hiding** для expired/disabled UI states — 4 fixes использовали disable+tooltip вместо hide (BOOKING-UI / RESCHEDULE / DASHBOARD / VK-NOTIFICATIONS)
  - **Boundary-preserving feature additions** — каждый fix усиливал, не ослаблял существующие invariants (#11 booking overlap / #25 master CRM privacy / #26 chat ACL все intact или усилены)
  - **HMAC opaque tokens** для privacy-sensitive URLs (chat attachments + client history) — reusable pattern для будущих signed-URL surfaces
  - **Defense layering** — backend stays as last-resort defense; UI prevents 99% of user-visible 409/403 errors via preemptive disable + explanatory tooltip

  Carryover (параллельные потоки):
  - 🔴 **EMAIL-VERIFY-FIX** (из CLIENT-SHOWCASE-SEED audit) — `email` пишется в @unique колонку до подтверждения → P2002 → 500. Pre-launch блокер.
  - 🟠 **OAUTH-FIX-A** (из CLIENT-SHOWCASE-SEED audit) — VK/TG button confusion в runtime; static-audit не подтвердил root cause, нужен live debug.
  - 🟡 **Manual tag assignment** (CLIENTS-FIX-A deferred) — UI ready, backend ready, hold pending product instinct change
  - 🟡 **VK notifications subsystem completion** (this fix's reverse) — когда delivery channel + queue handler доделаны, flip `NEXT_PUBLIC_VK_NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED=true`, флаг включит UI/endpoint автоматически
  - Other workstreams (client cabinet QA / studio QA fixes) — отдельные потоки, не блокированы master-волной

- **VK-NOTIFICATIONS-FLAG-A** — 8/8 (последний) commit master cabinet волны. **Audit-zero-code 9-й case**: VK push delivery channel НЕ существует в `notifications/delivery.ts` (только in-app / push / Telegram / email implemented). VkLink schema + OAuth callback + `VkNotificationsSection` UI (5 surfaces) + `/api/integrations/vk/settings` PATCH endpoint все существуют но указывают на несуществующий delivery → toggle создаёт no-op subscription. **Fix preemptive infrastructure**: (1) **`isVkNotificationsEnabled` computed flag** ([`env.ts`](src/lib/env.ts)) parallel to existing `isVkAuthEnabled` (login). String-coerced (`String(env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VK_NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED) === "true"`) для корректной работы и на сервере (Zod-parsed boolean), и на клиенте (raw process.env string fallback когда Zod parse fails missing non-public secrets like DATABASE_URL). Default false. (2) **Endpoint gate** ([`/api/integrations/vk/settings`](src/app/api/integrations/vk/settings/route.ts)) — `if (enabled && !isVkNotificationsEnabled) return ok({ enabled: false })`. Mirrors existing «silent skip when plan feature off» pattern (line 21-25). NO error thrown — intentional skip. (3) **UI gate** ([`vk-notifications.tsx`](src/features/cabinet/components/vk-notifications.tsx)) — Switch `disabled={saving || !isVkNotificationsEnabled}` + `title` tooltip + 2-line soft hint card («Мы дорабатываем доставку. Подключение к VK сохранится — уведомления включатся автоматически, когда канал заработает.»). Connect button preserved (login linking stays available). (4) **`.env.example`** updated с explicit comment about subsystem dependency. **VK login полностью НЕ тронут** — `start/callback/disable/status` endpoints unchanged; OAuth flow / session creation / role assignment preserved. **Other notification channels** (in-app / push / Telegram / email) не затронуты — flag только для VK delivery (которой и нет). **Existing `VkLink.isEnabled` field** preserved — flag не удаляет, только gates UX. **Reversal trivial**: когда delivery channel ships, `NEXT_PUBLIC_VK_NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED=true` в production env → UI toggle + endpoint автоматически работают без code change. **NO schema migration**. **NO FeatureFlag DB model** — простой env-based reuse существующего `boolFlag` + computed-flag pattern в env.ts (как `isVkAuthEnabled` / `isPushEnabled` / etc). 440 → **449 tests** (+9 flag predicate tests covering boolean / string / undefined / typo defence). lint baseline 1/3 preserved. typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅.

- **MASTER-MODELS-FIX-A** — 7/8 коммит master cabinet волны. Multi-application cascade fix + master CRM «откликался» marker. **Audit**: (1) **No `deleteMany`** в accept flow — user's worry was misplaced. Real bug: client-confirm endpoint ([`/api/model-applications/[id]/confirm`](src/app/api/model-applications/[applicationId]/confirm/route.ts)) flipped `offer.status → CLOSED` но НЕ касался sibling applications → они оставались в stale `PENDING` / `APPROVED_WAITING_CLIENT` на closed offer (ghost rows для master, «still waiting» для client). (2) **`closeOfferWithCascade` pattern** ([`model-offers-mutations.ts`](src/lib/master/model-offers-mutations.ts)) ALREADY does the right cascade (`PENDING/APPROVED_WAITING_CLIENT → REJECTED` + `notifyModelApplicationRejected` post-tx) when master manually closes offer — но confirm path обходил эту логику. (3) **`ModelApplicationStatus` enum**: PENDING/REJECTED/APPROVED_WAITING_CLIENT/CONFIRMED. **REJECTED semantically покрывает «not selected»** — это уже used `closeOfferWithCascade` для siblings → **Сценарий A confirmed, NO migration**. (4) **Client UX concern**: existing `client-model-applications-page.tsx:statusMeta` показывает «Отклонена / Мастер не принял заявку» для REJECTED — harsh для cascade-rejects. Derivation `offer.status === "CLOSED"` distinguishes cascade vs direct без schema change. (5) **Master CRM marker** — derived counter `modelApplicationsCount` on `ClientDetailView`, providerId-scoped (только offers данного master), private (invariant #25 enforced). **Fixes**: (1) **Confirm endpoint cascade** ([`confirm/route.ts`](src/app/api/model-applications/[applicationId]/confirm/route.ts)): inside Serializable tx, после `application.update({status: CONFIRMED})` и перед `offer.update({status: CLOSED})` — `findMany` siblings `(offerId === confirmedOffer && id !== self && status in [PENDING, APPROVED_WAITING_CLIENT])`, `updateMany` → REJECTED + null `proposedTimeLocal/confirmedStartAt`. Post-tx loop: `notifyModelApplicationRejected(sibling, "Выбран другой отклик")` для каждого cascaded — mirrors `closeOfferWithCascade` notification pattern. **`siblingCascadeIds` collected inside tx, notifications fire AFTER** — flaky notifier не откатывает booking creation. (2) **Client UX softening** ([`client-model-applications-page.tsx`](src/features/model-offers/components/client-model-applications-page.tsx)): `statusMeta()` signature extended с `offerStatus: string`. Derivation: `REJECTED + offer.status === "CLOSED"` → soft badge «Не выбран» + description «Мастер выбрал другого участника». Direct REJECTED on still-ACTIVE offer → existing «Отклонена / Мастер не принял заявку». Caller threads `item.offer.status`. NO API contract change (offer.status уже в DTO). (3) **Master CRM marker** in [`clients-view.service.ts`](src/lib/master/clients-view.service.ts): `ClientDetailView` extended с `modelApplicationsCount: number`. Single `prisma.modelApplication.count({ where: { clientUserId, offer: { masterId }, status: { not: "CONFIRMED" } } })` — cheap, master-private (scope by `offer.masterId`). Only registered clients have applications (`aggregate.clientUserId !== null`) — phone-only bookings get count = 0. (4) **Badge UI** ([`client-detail-header.tsx`](src/features/master/components/clients/client-detail-header.tsx)): distinct visual («Откликался на модельные» + `Sparkles` icon + `bg-brand-gradient` + `text-white`) — параллельно к `classifyClient` auto-status badges (не override bucket). Tooltip `«Клиент откликался на ваши модельные предложения N раз/раза/раз, но не был выбран. Контакт сохранён — можно пригласить снова.»` Local Russian pluralisation helper для count tail (раз/раза/раз). Visible only когда `modelApplicationsCount > 0`. **NO schema migration**. **NO existing endpoint contracts changed** — confirm endpoint behaviour extended additively. **flow.ts / chat / media / CHAT-FOUNDATION / RESCHEDULE / BOOKING-UI / DASHBOARD / CLIENTS / PRIVACY** все предыдущие fix-ы не тронуты. **`classifyClient` not modified** — параллельный marker, не bucket override. **`upsertClientCard` / notes endpoint (CLIENTS-FIX-A)** не тронуты — orthogonal CRM concept. **Edge cases handled**: ModelApplication `onDelete: Cascade` from ModelOffer relation preserved as designed (если offer удалён, applications удаляются — это не path этого fix); client-cancelled applications не существуют как concept в текущем enum (no CANCELLED status — клиенты не могут отозвать); expired offers (ARCHIVED) — пометка как «direct reject» в client UX (treat as legacy/end-of-life). **Tests**: 11 new (9 cascade predicate + 1 modelApplicationsCount privacy regression + 1 historyToken privacy regression on `ClientBookingDTO`). 429 → **440 tests** ✅. Existing 13 #25 privacy tests pass без изменений — `modelApplicationsCount` остаётся master-DTO only. UI_TEXT extended ~6 keys (`modelApplicantBadge`, `modelApplicantTooltipTemplate`, `modelApplicantPluralOne/Few/Many`). lint baseline 1/3 preserved, typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **Carryover**: 1 last master cabinet bug — **VK-NOTIFICATIONS-FLAG-A** (#8).
- **MASTER-CLIENTS-FIX-A** — 6/8 коммит master cabinet волны. 3 связанные изменения «Клиенты» surface: #7а URL без cuid + functional handler + #7в auto-status tooltips + #6 notes feature realisation (replace «Скоро» placeholder). Tags ручное → BACKLOG (auto-tagging confirmed). **Audit:** (a) **«Грубая ошибка» #7а** root cause: `/cabinet/master/bookings` route ([`page.tsx`](src/app/(cabinet)/cabinet/master/bookings/page.tsx)) принимал ТОЛЬКО `q` + `tab` — `?client=user:cuid` param от «Вся история» кнопки **игнорировался полностью**. URL вёл на страницу bookings но без фильтра → показывался весь kanban вместо истории выбранного клиента. PLUS privacy concern: raw cuid в URL. Два бага сразу — функциональный + privacy. (b) **Auto-tagging confirmed**: `classifyClient` ([`clients-classifier.ts`](src/lib/master/clients-classifier.ts)) derives 4 buckets (vip/regular/new/sleeping) из booking history. Pure derived data, не stored. Tag-editor placeholder («+ тег» disabled) → **manual tags → BACKLOG** per spec rule «есть авто → ручное в backlog». (c) **Canonical notes storage**: `ClientCard.notes` (master CRM card, providerId-scoped). Backend write path **already exists**: `PATCH /api/master/clients/[clientKey]/card` ([`route.ts`](src/app/api/master/clients/[clientKey]/card/route.ts)) с master-auth + `ensureClientCardAccess` plan-gate + Zod `clientCardPatchSchema` (max 2000 chars). **«Скоро» placeholder was UI gap only** — backend ready since CRM phase. (d) Privacy invariant #25 review: `ClientDetailView` includes `notes/tags/customTags` — master-only DTO returned via `/api/master/clients/[key]/detail`; never threaded into client DTOs. 13 existing privacy regression tests pass без изменений. **Fixes:** (1) **NEW** [`src/lib/master/client-key-token.ts`](src/lib/master/client-key-token.ts) — HMAC-signed opaque token (purpose `client-history-filter`, TTL 24h, master-scoped via `masterProviderId` in payload). Pattern mirrors CHAT-FOUNDATION attachment token. Uses `AUTH_JWT_SECRET`. Two pure functions: `signClientKeyToken({clientKey, masterProviderId})` + `verifyClientKeyToken({token, masterProviderId})`. 11 unit tests covering roundtrip / **URL-no-cuid** assertion / cross-master rejection / expiry / tampered body / tampered signature / malformed / cross-purpose replay (mismatched `purpose` claim rejected). (2) `ClientDetailView` extended with `historyToken: string` (server-pre-signed). Replaces raw `client.key` in [`client-detail-panel.tsx`](src/features/master/components/clients/client-detail-panel.tsx) Link href. (3) **Route handler fixed**: [`master-bookings-page.tsx`](src/features/master/components/bookings/master-bookings-page.tsx) reads `?client=<token>`, verifies via `verifyClientKeyToken` против current master, passes decoded `clientKey` в `KanbanFilters`. Invalid/expired tokens fall through silently (full kanban). (4) `KanbanFilters` extended с optional `clientKey`. `getMasterBookingsForKanban` ([`bookings.service.ts`](src/lib/master/bookings.service.ts)) builds Prisma `clientFilter` once (using existing `parseClientKeyIdentity` + `buildPhoneVariants` для phone-variant matching), applies к ОБОИМ buckets (active + cancelled) через `AND` composition. (5) **Tooltips #7в** added to `client.statuses` badges via native `title` attribute в [`client-detail-header.tsx`](src/features/master/components/clients/client-detail-header.tsx). 4 tooltip strings explaining `classifyClient` rules («VIP — от 50 000 ₽», «Постоянная — 5+ записей», «Новая — первый визит за 30 дней или ещё нет завершённых», «Спящая — был визит, но более 90 дней назад»). `cursor-help` style для UX cue. Disabled «+ тег» button tooltip updated с «Скоро» → «Категории назначаются автоматически…» — honest UX, объясняет почему disabled (не «фича в разработке», а «уже есть автоматическое»). `STATUS_TONES` type refactored to use `ClientStatus` directly (was tied to STATUS_T keys — broke когда I added `tooltips` subkey). (6) **NEW** [`src/features/master/components/clients/client-notes-editor.tsx`](src/features/master/components/clients/client-notes-editor.tsx) — full edit-mode UI: read-mode shows preview + «Редактировать» CTA; edit-mode shows `<Textarea>` + char counter (`{N} / 2000`) + Save / Cancel buttons + loading state + error display + Escape-to-cancel keyboard shortcut. Calls existing `PATCH /api/master/clients/[clientKey]/card` с `{ notes }` body. Optimistic state update with rollback on failure. Replaces orphan `ClientNotesDisplay` (deleted). (7) **Privacy regression**: existing `client-privacy.test.ts` (13 tests) pass без изменений — `notes` поле остаётся в master-only DTO (`ClientDetailView`); никаких изменений в client-DTOs. Token also tested to NEVER contain raw clientKey в base64-encoded body (test assertion). **NO schema migration**. **NO backend endpoint changes** — only consumed existing `PATCH /api/master/clients/[clientKey]/card`. **NO new endpoints**. **flow.ts / chat / media / CHAT-FOUNDATION / RESCHEDULE / BOOKING-UI / DASHBOARD предыдущие fix-ы** — не тронуты. **Invariant #25 not modified** — наполнен реальной фичей, защита сохранена. Schema (ClientCard.notes existing field) reused. **Tags scope**: manual tag-editor в **🟡 BACKLOG** — auto-tagging covers все 4 buckets, manual tags не имеют distinguishing value сейчас. **Tests**: 418 → **429 tests** (+11 token sign/verify/boundary). UI_TEXT extended ~15 ключами (`status.autoHint`, `status.tooltips.{new,regular,vip,sleeping}`, `notes.{editPlaceholder,saveLabel,saving,cancelLabel,saveError}`, `addTagDisabled` updated). lint baseline 1/3 preserved, typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅. **Carryover**: 2 master cabinet bugs остаются (MASTER-MODELS-FIX-A #8 + VK-NOTIFICATIONS-FLAG-A)
- **MASTER-DASHBOARD-FIX-A** — 3 связанные dashboard-проблемы под общей темой «состояние во времени». **#1а Attention shows past bookings** — root cause: `getPendingBookingsForMaster` (master-pending-list.ts) фильтровал по статусу + role (BOOKING-UI-FIX-A correctly added `actionRequiredBy: MASTER` for PENDING+CHANGE_REQUESTED) но не исключал `startAtUtc <= now`. Запись чей старт уже наступил больше не actionable, но `orderBy startAtUtc asc` пододвигал её в первую очередь. **Fix**: добавлен `startAtUtc: { gt: now }` в where (gt also excludes legacy null startAtUtc — корректно, slot-label-only записи не время-bound). **#1б Hydration error** — root cause (audit-driven, не угаданный): [`manual-booking-modal.tsx:25`](src/features/master/components/dashboard/manual-booking-modal.tsx#L25) `todayDateKey()` (`new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)`) вызывался в `useState` initial value на line 44 — server-time SSR vs client-time hydration produced разные date strings вокруг полуночи / разных timezones → React hydration mismatch. **Fix**: initial state стартует пустым, mount-gated useEffect ставит today's default только после hydration (детерминированный SSR render). **#3 Expired actions still active** — backend `ensureBookingActionWindow` (flow.ts) уже rejects cancel/reschedule с 409 «менее 60 минут до старта», но UI render оставлял кнопки полностью активными. **Fix**: новый shared helper [`src/lib/bookings/action-state.ts`](src/lib/bookings/action-state.ts) — `isBookingPastConfirmWindow` (`start <= now`) + `isBookingPastModifyWindow` (`minutesUntilStart < BOOKING_ACTION_WINDOW_MINUTES`). Оба reuse canonical `flow.ts` semantics — `BOOKING_ACTION_WINDOW_MINUTES = 60` + `minutesUntilStart` — никакой re-implementation. Применён в **4 action sites** with **visibility-over-hiding** (disable + tooltip, не hide): `dashboard/booking-row-actions.tsx` (icon row — reschedule + cancel disabled when past modify window); `dashboard/booking-row.tsx` + `dashboard/booking-action-buttons.tsx` (confirm/decline disabled when past confirm window); `bookings/booking-manage-actions.tsx` (kanban — both reschedule + cancel disabled when past modify window); `schedule/booking-card-actions-menu.tsx` (popover — confirm/decline + reschedule + cancel все respect their windows). `MenuItem` принимает новый `title` prop для tooltips. **Tooltip strings** — 2 новых ключа в `cabinetMaster.dashboard.bookings.{modifyWindowExpiredTooltip, confirmWindowExpiredTooltip}` reused across 4 sites — single source of truth. **NO schema migration. NO flow.ts changes (32 tests preserved). NO backend `createBooking`/`approveChangeRequest`/`rejectChangeRequest` modifications.** MASTER-BOOKING-UI-FIX-A role-aware logic (`actionRequiredBy`) preserved + extended coherently. MASTER-RESCHEDULE-FIX-A guards preserved. Invariants #11/#25/#26 не пересекают. `resolveChatAccess` / CHAT-FOUNDATION / privacy не тронуты. **Tests**: 13 new unit tests for action-state predicates (boundary at 60min, null/invalid input, mutual relationship between confirm + modify windows). 405 → **418 tests** ✅. UI_TEXT расширен 2 ключами. lint baseline 1/3 preserved, typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅
- **MASTER-BOOKING-UI-FIX-A** — 2 связанные UI-проблемы master booking actions: #2а initiator-aware action visibility + #2б browser native dialogs → ModalSurface. **Audit:** (1) #2а root cause — `Booking.actionRequiredBy` enum уже existed в schema (set by `usecases.ts:rescheduleBooking` line 226 to opposite-of-actor), but **3 master-side service DTOs не surface'или это поле** (`ScheduleBookingItem` / `DashboardBooking` / `KanbanBookingItem`) — поэтому UI рендерил confirm/decline buttons для **обеих** сторон CHANGE_REQUESTED брони, и клик инициатора получал backend 409 «Action is required from another side». (2) #2б audit — 5 native `window.prompt(...)` calls в booking flow: `booking-card-actions.tsx` (decline reason) / `booking-manage-actions.tsx` (cancel reason) / `dashboard/booking-action-buttons.tsx` (decline reason) / `schedule/booking-card-actions-menu.tsx` (decline + cancel). Existing `useConfirm`/`ConfirmModal` — yes/no only, не подходит для text-input prompt. **Fixes:** (1) **NEW primitives** [`src/components/ui/prompt-modal.tsx`](src/components/ui/prompt-modal.tsx) + [`src/hooks/use-prompt.tsx`](src/hooks/use-prompt.tsx) — text-input аналог `ConfirmModal`/`useConfirm`. Imperative API `const { prompt, modal } = usePrompt(); const reason = await prompt({...});`. Required validation (trimmed), maxLength, danger variant, async-safe loading. Same patterns as existing useConfirm — consistent reuse. (2) **DTOs extended** с `actionRequiredBy: "CLIENT" | "MASTER" | null` — `ScheduleBookingItem` ([schedule.service.ts](src/lib/master/schedule.service.ts)), `DashboardBooking` ([dashboard.service.ts](src/lib/master/dashboard.service.ts)), `KanbanBookingItem` ([bookings.service.ts](src/lib/master/bookings.service.ts)). Prisma selects обогащены. (3) **Role-gated actions in 4 UI sites**: `booking-card-actions-menu.tsx` (schedule) splits `canConfirmOrDecline = isPendingNew || (isAwaitingResponse && actionRequiredBy === "MASTER")`, renders «Запрос переноса отправлен — ждём ответ клиента» hint when `isInitiatorWaitingResponse`; `booking-card-actions.tsx` (kanban pending) early-returns guard view for initiator; `dashboard/booking-row.tsx` gates `<BookingActionButtons>` rendering with same predicate; `booking-manage-actions.tsx` (kanban confirmed/today) already had RESCHEDULE-FIX guard (Reschedule disabled). (4) **5 native prompts → usePrompt** with branded ModalSurface dialogs: decline и cancel reasons with title/label/placeholder/confirmLabel/danger variant from new UI_TEXT keys. (5) **reschedule-context endpoint** extended with `actionRequiredBy` (для future "Отозвать запрос" feature если бизнес добавит cancel-pending). **NO schema migration** (initiator data already existed). **NO backend changes** (rescheduleBooking / approve/reject / createBooking / flow.ts — все нетронуты; backend остаётся защитой last resort). **NO new dialog component besides PromptModal** — это правильный sibling к ConfirmModal (yes-no vs text-input), не overlap. Invariants #11/#25/#26 не пересекают. **CHAT-FOUNDATION** не тронут. **3 client-side reschedule UIs + RESCHEDULE guards** не сломаны. **Tests**: 11 new (5 prompt validation contract + 6 role-visibility predicate). 394 → **405 tests** ✅. UI_TEXT расширен ~13 ключами (`common.commentLabel`; `cabinetMaster.schedule.bookingCard.{awaitingClientResponse, declineTitle/Label/Placeholder/ConfirmLabel, cancelTitle/Label/Placeholder/ConfirmLabel}`; `cabinetMaster.bookings.card.{awaitingClientResponse, cancelTitle/Label/Placeholder/ConfirmLabel, declineTitle/Label/Placeholder/ConfirmLabel}`; `cabinetMaster.dashboard.bookingActions.{declineTitle/Label/Placeholder/ConfirmLabel}`). lint baseline preserved, typecheck/build/encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅
- **MASTER-RESCHEDULE-FIX-A** — 2 связанные проблемы переноса броней в master cabinet (#5а pending-block UI + #5б free-slots-only) + bonus enforcement. **Audit findings:** 4 reschedule UI surfaces (master schedule modal, master kanban actions, master dashboard actions, client cabinet modal, client-cabinet section). **3 of 4 уже использовали free-slots endpoints** (`client-cabinet/bookings/client-reschedule-modal.tsx` → `/api/public/providers/{id}/slots`, `cabinet/components/reschedule-modal.tsx` + `reschedule-section.tsx` → `/api/masters/{id}/availability`). **Только `master/components/schedule/reschedule-modal.tsx` использовал raw `<input type="date">/<input type="time">`** — exact source #5б bug. **#5а pending-block detection**: `BookingDto.status === "CHANGE_REQUESTED"` уже surfaced через `mappers.ts:toBookingDto → resolveBookingRuntimeStatus` (TEST-COVERAGE flow.ts). Backend в `usecases.ts:rescheduleBooking` уже 409'ит pending (line 139-146); UI просто не знал — `booking-card-actions-menu.tsx:160` объединял `isPending = PENDING || CHANGE_REQUESTED` для reschedule action visibility. **Bonus**: `assertBookingWindow` (BOOKING-WIDGET-A) НЕ применялся в reschedule — добавлено reuse policy-enforcement (booking-window правила теперь системно-консистентны: createBooking + rescheduleBooking). **Fixes:** (1) new lightweight `GET /api/master/bookings/[id]/reschedule-context` endpoint — auth-gated (current master must own booking), returns `{masterProviderId, serviceId, durationMin, status}` lazy-loaded by modal on open. (2) Full rewrite [`master/components/schedule/reschedule-modal.tsx`](src/features/master/components/schedule/reschedule-modal.tsx): replaces raw date/time inputs with date-chips (14 days) + `SlotPickerOptimized` shared primitive, fed by reused `/api/masters/{id}/availability` (same endpoint other 3 UIs use). On open: fetch context → if `status === "CHANGE_REQUESTED"` render «В ожидании» guard view (no submit), else render free-slots picker. **Excludes current slot** from picker via `originalIso` filter (можно перенести на тот же слот не имеет смысла). Loading/error/empty states for both context and slot fetches. (3) Caller guards (3 sites): `booking-card-actions-menu.tsx` splits `isPending` into `isPendingNew` (PENDING only) + `isAwaitingResponse` (CHANGE_REQUESTED), reschedule visible только for `isPendingNew || isConfirmed`; confirm/decline остаётся в обоих pending states. `booking-manage-actions.tsx` (kanban) accepts new `status` prop, gates Reschedule button с tooltip «Уже есть запрос переноса в ожидании ответа». `dashboard/booking-row-actions.tsx` (dashboard icon row) hides Reschedule when `status === "CHANGE_REQUESTED"`. Caller `booking-card.tsx` passes `booking.rawStatus`. (4) Backend [`usecases.ts:rescheduleBooking`](src/lib/bookings/usecases.ts): provider select extended with `minBookingHoursAhead` + `maxBookingDaysAhead`, `assertBookingWindow(input.startAtUtc, booking.provider, now)` called после `ensureBookingActionWindow`. Same try/catch → Result pattern as other policy guards. `acceptNewClients` НЕ re-checked (returning client by definition has booking with this provider). **3 client-side reschedule UIs** (client cabinet + cabinet modal + cabinet section) **не тронуты** — они уже корректны (audit-confirmed). **NO schema migration. NO booking core / createBooking / overlap #11 / flow.ts state-machine modifications.** `resolveChatAccess` / chat / invariant #25 / #26 — не пересекают. Существующие 4 reschedule API endpoints — unchanged contract. policy-enforcement reused, не модифицирован. **Tests**: new `reschedule-enforcement.test.ts` (4 tests: too-soon → BOOKING_TOO_SOON 400, too-far → BOOKING_TOO_FAR 400, per-provider policy values applied, pending-guard precedence preserved). UI text expanded с 9 новыми ключами (`durationLabel`, `contextLoading/Error`, `slotsLoading/Error`, `noSlots`, `pendingTitle/Body`, `rescheduleAwaitingTooltip`). 390 → **394 tests** ✅. lint baseline preserved, typecheck/encoding/mojibake/prisma/build ✅
- **MASTER-CHAT-ATTACHMENT-FIX-A** — 3 связанные проблемы chat attachment в master cabinet, **общая первопричина: ACL не покрывал `MediaEntityType.CHAT_MESSAGE`**. Один fix решил все 3 (плюс закрыл cuid-leak в URL). **Audit root cause:** [`src/lib/media/access.ts:ensureCanReadMedia`](src/lib/media/access.ts) `kindAllowedForEntity` switch не имел case для `CHAT_MESSAGE` → `default: false` → throw 403 для **каждого** запроса chat attachment, включая sender. Браузер при 403 на `<img>` рендерит broken-icon placeholder ≈24px высотой — это и была «полоска» (#4а). #4б (403) и #4а (полоска) — одна и та же баг. #4в (cuid в URL) — параллельный privacy concern. **Fixes**: (1) `ensureCanReadMedia` extended с `CHAT_MESSAGE` case + новый helper `canReadChatAttachmentMedia` который парсит `chat-message:<msgId>` из `entityId`, finds ChatMessage → BookingChat → Booking, admits только chat participants (client + master); studio admin и outsider → 403 (privacy 152-ФЗ invariant, by-design). Availability gate intentionally skipped для read (участники должны видеть историю после окончания брони). (2) Новый opaque-token URL scheme в [`src/lib/media/private-delivery.ts`](src/lib/media/private-delivery.ts): `createChatAttachmentToken(assetId) → HMAC-signed base64url payload {aid, exp:15min, purpose:"chat-attachment-read"}`. URL pattern `/api/chat/attachment/[token]` — **никаких cuid в URL** (test asserts `url.contains(assetId) === false`). Distinct `purpose` claim предотвращает cross-replay с generic `media-read` токенами. (3) Новый endpoint [`/api/chat/attachment/[token]/route.ts`](src/app/api/chat/attachment/[token]/route.ts) — decode token → verify expiry + signature → `getMediaFile(user, assetId)` (который вызывает `ensureCanReadMedia`). (4) `ThreadMessageDto.attachmentMediaAssetId` → `ThreadMessageDto.attachmentUrl: string | null` — server-built signed URL вместо raw cuid. `conversation-aggregator.ts` строит URL через `buildChatAttachmentUrl(assetId)` когда у message есть attachment. Тип ThreadMessage в `thread-grouping.ts` обновлён аналогично. (5) `message-bubble.tsx` rewrite: `AttachmentImage` component с aspect-ratio `4/3` + `min-h-[200px]` + skeleton (bg placeholder пока грузится) + error state (ImageOff icon + «Не удалось загрузить вложение») — даже если bytes не приходят, UI не схлопывается в полоску. **CHAT-FOUNDATION** (SSE/receipts/sendConversationMessage) **не тронут**. `resolveChatAccess` **не модифицирован** — reused его membership logic в новом helper (studio admin denial 152-ФЗ инвариант сохранён). **Schema не тронута**. **Invariant #25 (privacy master CRM notes)** не пересекает — отдельная плоскость. Существующие attachments в БД работают (DTO заново строит токен на каждом fetch). 371 → 390 tests (+19: 11 token + 8 ACL boundary). **Файлы изменены**: 6 src + 2 новых test + 1 новый route. Рабочий код 0 регрессий: typecheck/build/encoding/mojibake/prisma ✅, lint baseline 1/3 preserved
- **MASTER-PRIVACY-FIX-A** — privacy boundary для master CRM private fields (`Booking.notes`, `ClientCard.notes/tags/photos`, `ClientNote.text`). **Audit-zero-code результат** (CHAT-FOUNDATION pattern): boundary уже корректно соблюдён в коде на момент аудита — ни одно client-facing место (`src/lib/bookings/list.ts` `listClientBookings`, `src/lib/client-cabinet/bookings.service.ts`, `/api/cabinet/user/*`, `/api/bookings/my`, `/api/bookings/[id]/chat`, client-cabinet UI компоненты) не selects/exposes master-private поля. Все client DTO (`BookingDto`, `BookingClientDto`, `ClientBookingDTO`) имеют explicit-list select без `notes/tags/clientCard`. **Добавлены 13 privacy regression tests** ([`src/lib/bookings/client-privacy.test.ts`](src/lib/bookings/client-privacy.test.ts)) — double-layer: (1) type-level assertions (`"notes" extends keyof BookingClientDto ? true : false` — compile fail если ключ просочится в тип); (2) source-level checks (читает 6 client-facing files и assert'ит отсутствие `notes: true`, `clientCard:`, `clientNote:` regex pattern'ов — ловит даже когда `as`-cast обходит types). **Master CRM** (`src/lib/master/*`, `src/lib/studio/*` где notes легитимны) — не тронут. **Schema не тронута** — поля остаются (master их пишет/читает как раньше). **Новый инвариант #25 в AI_CONTEXT р.12** (152-ФЗ контекст). 358 → 371 tests (+13). Note: user формулировка «вижу что заметки мастера недоступны для клиента — стоит добавить» интерпретируется как «boundary корректен, **зафиксировать инвариант + regression защиту**» — что и сделано

Содержит: User+UserProfile, 9 bookings разных статусов у Анны Соколовой (CONFIRMED/PENDING/CHANGE_REQUESTED/3×FINISHED/2×CANCELLED/NO_SHOW), 3 reviews (5★+5★+4★) на FINISHED брони (2 с ответом мастера / 1 без), favorite Анны (rebook surface), 5 notifications (3 unread + 2 read, разные NotificationType), 1 PENDING ModelApplication если у Анны есть ACTIVE offer. Идемпотентность: phone-first upsert через `ensureUserByPhone` + deterministic seed IDs (`seed-bk-showcase-client-NN`, `seed-notif-showcase-client-NN`). Расширен `SHOWCASE_PHONE_PREFIXES` (+`+79995`) + новый константный `SHOWCASE_PHONE_CLIENT` → `reset.ts` подхватывает client showcase автоматически. **Аудит #1 email-500** (НЕ исправлено): root cause = `request-verify/route.ts:72-75` пишет `email` сразу в `@unique` колонку до подтверждения, P2002 при коллизии → 500. **Заметка #2 OAuth путаница** (НЕ исправлено): static-audit не подтверждает root cause, нужен runtime-аудит на login page. Оба бага занесены в BACKLOG (🔴 #1 / 🟠 #2) с детальными fix options для отдельных промптов EMAIL-VERIFY-FIX и OAUTH-FIX-A. **Рабочий код 0 изменений** — только seed (markers.ts + index.ts + новый seed-showcase-client.ts) + BACKLOG + AI_CONTEXT (CLIENT-SHOWCASE-SEED-A)

  - **Refactor:** `calculateMRR()` перемещён из `src/features/admin-cabinet/billing/lib/mrr.ts` → `src/lib/billing/mrr.ts` (worker не должен зависеть от features-слоя)
  - Новый `src/lib/billing/mrr-snapshot.ts`: `createMrrSnapshotForToday()` (idempotent, race-safe via P2002 fallback re-read), `getMrrSnapshotDaysAgo()` (no nearest-neighbour fallback by design)
  - Новый queue job type `mrr.snapshot.daily` (zero payload) + worker handler `processMrrSnapshotDailyJob` (использует общий retry/dead-letter mechanism)
  - Новый endpoint `POST /api/billing/mrr/snapshot/run` — auth через `x-cron-token` header (same pattern as `/api/billing/renew/run`), validates against `MRR_SNAPSHOT_SECRET` env var, enqueues job and returns fast
  - **KPI integration:** `getAdminBillingKpis()` теперь читает snapshot ~30 дней назад и вычисляет `mrr.deltaPercent` через BigInt arithmetic. `null` → UI рендерит «—»
  - Unit tests: 12 tests across `mrr.test.ts` (6) + `mrr-snapshot.test.ts` (6) — UTC date truncation, idempotency, race fallback, missing-price-row handling, BigInt math
  - Runbook `docs/runbooks/mrr-snapshot-cron.md` — endpoint usage, cron schedule recommendation (02:00 UTC), failure modes, backfill note
  - Env: новый `MRR_SNAPSHOT_SECRET` в `env.ts` + `.env.example`

  - **Subscriptions tab:** таблица ACTIVE+PAST_DUE подписок с 7 columns (user / plan / since / next / amount / method / autoRenew). Cursor pagination. Cancel action через `POST /api/admin/billing/subscriptions/[id]/cancel` (новый endpoint)
  - **Cancel subscription endpoint** — новый. Semantics: `cancelAtPeriodEnd: true` + `autoRenew: false` + `cancelledAt: now` (user retains access until period end). Атомарная транзакция: subscription update + `BillingAuditLog.action="ADMIN_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED"` (с adminUserId + previousStatus + reason in details). Notification user'у через existing `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED` type (no admin-specific enum value)
  - **Payments tab:** pending + history groups. Pending — все PENDING, warning amber background. History — SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELED/REFUNDED, cursor-paginated. 5 payment statuses with distinct tones (success/warning/destructive/muted/info)
  - **Refund action** — reuses existing `POST /api/admin/billing/refund` endpoint, **extended** to accept `reason` body field (stored в audit log details). Endpoint already has idempotency via YooKassa idempotenceKey
  - **N+1 prevention:** subscriptions list includes `payments: { take: 1, where: SUCCEEDED, orderBy: createdAt desc }` for payment-method extraction. Payment method derived from `metadata.payment_method.title` Json blob best-effort; `null` → UI «—»
  - **Legacy `src/features/admin/components/admin-billing.tsx` УДАЛЁН** (1253 строки). User-approved deletion. После переключения page → no remaining importers
  - `BillingPaymentStatus` все 5 значений отображаются в UI (PENDING/SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELED/REFUNDED)
  - Refund button скрыт для non-refundable payments (`isRefundable = SUCCEEDED && has yookassaPaymentId`)
- **ADMIN-REVIEWS-A** (2026-05-13) — новый SSR-driven UI для `/admin/reviews` под admin-cabinet shell. Sub-features:
  - Header (caption «Модерация отзывов и жалоб» + «N жалоб ожидают» indicator); без правого CTA «Фильтры» per spec
  - 4 KPI tiles: pendingReports (count + urgent count в красном — `rating=1 OR reason=OFFENSIVE`), reviewsToday (count + delta vs 7-day rolling avg), averageRating (всё-temp arithmetic mean over non-deleted), deletedLastWeek (**null** — нет `Review.deletedAt`, UI рендерит «—»). Tone resolvers: ok/warn/danger/neutral per tile
  - 3 tabs (Все / С жалобами / Низкий рейтинг ≤2) + debounced search (200 мс) по text/reportComment/author.displayName. URL state через `?tab=`/`?q=`/`?cursor=`. Tab counts вычислены параллельно с `Promise.all`
  - Review cards (3-column grid на desktop, stack на mobile): main (author masked + target + stars + text + reply) / actions (Approve если reported + Delete всегда) / report info (single reason label + comment, **no** breakdown counts per spec — multi-reporter в backlog)
  - **Author privacy** — централизованный helper `maskAuthorDisplay()`: «Алексей Иванов» → «Алексей И.», single word fallback к initial-only
  - **Approve action** — clears `reportedAt`/`reportReason`/`reportComment`, audit via `logInfo("admin.reviews.approved", { adminUserId, reviewId, targetType, targetId })`. Throws `AdminApproveReviewError` с кодами `REVIEW_NOT_FOUND` (404) или `NOT_REPORTED` (400, idempotency-protection)
  - **Delete action** — **hard delete** + `recalculateTargetRatings(tx, targetType, targetId)` в атомарной транзакции. Audit via `logInfo("admin.reviews.deleted", { adminUserId, reviewId, reason, targetType, targetId })`. User-approved continuation legacy semantics; soft-delete migration **🔴 pre-launch blocker** в backlog
  - Confirmation dialogs: ApproveReviewDialog (минимал, без reason), DeleteReviewDialog (warning + опциональный `reason` textarea, danger button). Оба используют `ModalSurface` primitive (Portal-to-body)
  - 2 новых endpoints: `POST /api/admin/reviews/[id]/approve` (Zod-валидация на `id`, проверка auth через `requireAdminAuth`), `POST /api/admin/reviews/[id]/delete` (body `{reason?: string trim max 500}`)

  - Lib helpers: `urgency.ts` (`isUrgentReport`), `report-reason-display.ts` (`reportReasonLabel` switch over 5 enum values), `author-mask.ts` (`maskAuthorDisplay`)
  - Optimistic UI updates с `router.refresh()` после server confirmation

  - Cursor-based pagination через `?cursor=` + «Загрузить ещё» CTA (URL-driven, server-fetched)

  - **Legacy PATCH /api/admin/reviews/[id]** (action: dismiss_report) + **DELETE /api/admin/reviews/[id]** — оставлены functional для backwards-compat, не тронуты. Новый UI использует только новые dedicated routes

- **MIGRATIONS-PRELAUNCH-A** (2026-05-13) — foundation коммит pre-launch batch (1/4). **Только schema additions + types**, никаких behavior changes. Sub-features:
  - Schema migration `20260513224252_pre_launch_audit_soft_delete_block`:
    - **`AdminAuditLog` модель** в новом `prisma/schema/audit.prisma`: id / adminUserId(FK→UserProfile onDelete: Restrict) / action / targetType / targetId / details(Json) / reason / ipAddress / userAgent / createdAt. 4 индекса (adminUserId+createdAt DESC, targetType+targetId+createdAt DESC, action+createdAt DESC, createdAt DESC)
    - **`AdminAuditAction` enum** в `prisma/schema/enums.prisma`: 25 значений (`USER_PLAN_GRANTED`, `USER_BLOCKED`, `USER_UNBLOCKED`, `USER_ROLE_ADDED`, `USER_ROLE_REMOVED`, `USER_ACCOUNT_DELETED`, `BILLING_PLAN_EDITED`, `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED`, `BILLING_PAYMENT_REFUNDED`, `CITY_CREATED`, `CITY_UPDATED`, `CITY_DELETED`, `CITY_MERGED`, `CITY_VERIFIED`, `CATEGORY_APPROVED`, `CATEGORY_REJECTED`, `CATEGORY_EDITED`, `REVIEW_APPROVED`, `REVIEW_DELETED`, `REVIEW_RESTORED`, `SETTINGS_LOGO_UPDATED`, `SETTINGS_LOGIN_HERO_UPDATED`, `SETTINGS_SEO_UPDATED`, `SETTINGS_FLAG_TOGGLED`, `SETTINGS_APP_SETTING_UPDATED`)
    - **`Review` table extended**: `deletedAt? DateTime`, `deletedByUserId? String` (FK→UserProfile onDelete: SetNull, relation `ReviewDeletedBy`), `deletedReason? String`. Index `Review_deletedAt_idx`
    - **`UserProfile` table extended**: `blockedAt? DateTime`, `blockedByUserId? String` (self-FK onDelete: SetNull, relation `UserBlockedBy`), `blockedReason? String`. Index `UserProfile_blockedAt_idx`. Также добавлены back-relations `adminAuditLogs AdminAuditLog[]` + `reviewsDeleted Review[]` + `blockedUsers UserProfile[]`
  - **`BillingAuditLog` НЕ затронут** — продолжает работать параллельно. Retirement policy решается в **ADMIN-AUDIT-INTEGRATION** коммите после миграции существующих consumer'ов на `AdminAuditLog`
  - **Type module** `src/lib/audit/types.ts`: re-exports `AdminAuditAction` + `AdminAuditLog`, type `AdminAuditDetails` (diff-based + free-form), const map `ADMIN_AUDIT_ACTIONS` с `satisfies Record<AdminAuditAction, AdminAuditAction>` (exhaustiveness check)
  - **Behavior preserved:** existing endpoints **не тронуты**. Review delete по-прежнему hard delete (новые поля ignored). User block недоступен через API (поля nullable, не используются). `AdminAuditLog` пустой (no inserts yet)
  - Migration сгенерирована manual SQL (DB local unavailable), pattern точно совпадает с `multi_city_foundation` migration. `npx prisma validate` ✅, `npx prisma generate` ✅, typecheck ✅, 178/178 tests ✅, lint baseline сохранён (588 errors / 87 warnings)
  - Counts: models 64 → 65, enums 35 → 36, migrations 14 → 15
  - **Next batch:** ADMIN-AUDIT-INTEGRATION (2/4) → NOTIFICATION-TYPES-A (3/4) → REVIEW-SOFT-DELETE-A (4/4)
- **ADMIN-AUDIT-INTEGRATION** (2026-05-13) — pre-launch batch коммит 2 из 4. Перевод всех admin mutations на `AdminAuditLog` с capture IP + User-Agent. Sub-features:
  - Новый модуль `src/lib/audit/`:
    - `admin-audit.ts` — `createAdminAuditLog(input)` (strict, throws — use inside transactions) + `createAdminAuditLogSafe(input)` (catches and `logError`s — use outside transactions where audit loss is preferable to surfacing a 500)
    - `admin-audit-context.ts` — `getAdminAuditContext(req)` extracts IP via existing `getClientIp` helper + User-Agent (truncated to 512 chars). Best-effort: nullable. `EMPTY_ADMIN_AUDIT_CONTEXT` для service layers без request
    - `admin-audit-diff.ts` — `buildAdminAuditDiff<T>(before, after)` returns `{[K]: {before, after}}` для changed keys only. Equality: strict `===` для primitives, JSON-stringify для objects/arrays. `hasAnyDiff()` helper для conditional audit writes
    - `admin-audit-diff.test.ts` — 12 unit tests covering: no-op, primitive/boolean/null changes, undefined handling, multi-field diffs, nested objects, arrays, empty diff check. **190/190 tests passing**
  - **Group A — Billing (dual-write with BillingAuditLog):**
    - `src/app/api/admin/billing/plans/[id]/route.ts` PATCH — добавлен `BILLING_PLAN_EDITED` write **внутри** existing transaction рядом с `BillingAuditLog`. Diff передаётся в `details.changes`. Context: `getAdminAuditContext(req)`
    - `src/features/admin-cabinet/billing/server/cancel-subscription.service.ts` — service signature расширена `context?: AdminAuditContext`. Audit `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED` внутри tx + reason field
    - `src/app/api/admin/billing/refund/route.ts` POST — **`createAdminAuditLogSafe`** (вне tx, после YooKassa side-effect — failure не должен surface как 500). Audit `BILLING_PAYMENT_REFUNDED` + reason
    - `src/features/admin-cabinet/users/server/plan-change.service.ts` — service signature расширена `context?`. Audit `USER_PLAN_GRANTED` внутри tx с before/after план info
  - **Group B — Cities (logInfo → AdminAuditLog migration):**
    - `cities/route.ts` POST — wrap city.create в tx + audit `CITY_CREATED`. Context передаётся
    - `cities/[id]/route.ts` PATCH — **двойная audit** logic: `CITY_VERIFIED` если `autoCreated: true → false` toggle + `CITY_UPDATED` если other fields touched. Оба внутри одной tx
    - `cities/[id]/route.ts` DELETE — wrap delete в tx + audit `CITY_DELETED`. Param `_req → req` (был unused)
    - `cities/[id]/merge/route.ts` — audit `CITY_MERGED` внутри existing tx. targetId = surviving city, sourceCitySlug в details
  - **Group B — Catalog:**
    - `catalog/categories/[id]/approve/route.ts` POST — wrap update в tx + audit `CATEGORY_APPROVED`. Param `_req → req`
    - `catalog/categories/[id]/reject/route.ts` POST — wrap update + portfolioItem.updateMany в tx + audit `CATEGORY_REJECTED` + reason. Existing `logInfo` сохранён
    - `catalog/categories/[id]/route.ts` PATCH — wrap update + conditional portfolioItem update + audit `CATEGORY_EDITED` (fieldsChanged list per spec table — no diff values for sparse change)
  - **Group B — Reviews:**
    - `approve-review.service.ts` — wrap review.update в tx + audit `REVIEW_APPROVED` с previousReason. Service signature: `context?`
    - `delete-review.service.ts` — audit `REVIEW_DELETED` внутри existing tx (review.delete + recalculateTargetRatings + audit все вместе). Service signature: `context?`
    - Routes `reviews/[id]/{approve,delete}` обновлены: pass `context: getAdminAuditContext(req)`
  - **Group B — Settings:**
    - `system-config/route.ts` PATCH — рефакторинг: все upserts перенесены **внутрь tx**, **N audit entries per toggled key** (`SETTINGS_FLAG_TOGGLED` × {onlinePayments / visualSearch / legalDraft}). Cache invalidation после tx (best-effort). `logInfo` сохранён
    - `settings/route.ts` PATCH (SEO) — рефакторинг writeSetting → `writeSettingTx` (transaction client variant). Audit `SETTINGS_SEO_UPDATED` с full diff `{seoTitle?, seoDescription?}`. `logInfo` сохранён
    - `app-settings/route.ts` PATCH — wrap upsert в tx + audit `SETTINGS_APP_SETTING_UPDATED` с before/after если value реально изменилось
  - **NOT instrumented in this commit (logo / login-hero):** SETTINGS_LOGO_UPDATED + SETTINGS_LOGIN_HERO_UPDATED enum values добавлены в MIGRATIONS-PRELAUNCH-A но dedicated admin endpoints для upload **не существуют** — фото идут через generic `/api/media` POST в `src/lib/media/service.ts` (entityType=SITE). Audit там потребует instrumentation на уровне media service с entity-type filter. Tracked in backlog
  - **logInfo НЕ удалён** — secondary in-memory stream сохранён для debugging
  - **BillingAuditLog НЕ тронут** — forever-parallel decision documented. 4 dual-write sites продолжают писать в обе таблицы
  - **Counts:** ~16 endpoints instrumented (4 billing dual-write + 4 cities + 3 catalog + 2 reviews + 3 settings). 4 новых файла в `src/lib/audit/`. 12 новых tests
  - **Validation:** typecheck ✅, lint 588/87 (baseline preserved), encoding/mojibake ✅, 190/190 tests passing
  - **Next batch:** NOTIFICATION-TYPES-A (3/4) → REVIEW-SOFT-DELETE-A (4/4)
- **NOTIFICATION-TYPES-A** (2026-05-14) — pre-launch batch 3 из 4. Admin-initiated NotificationType enum extension + 3-канальный dispatcher + mass fan-out. Sub-features:

  - **Новые файлы (3 production + 1 test):**
    - `src/lib/notifications/admin-body-templates.ts` — pure body builders (`buildPlanGrantedBody`, `buildPlanEditedSummary` с smart sparse-diff filtering, `buildSubscriptionCancelledByAdminBody`, `buildRefundBody`, `buildReviewDeletedByAdminBody`) + push truncation helpers (`truncatePushBody` → 200 chars, `truncatePushTitle` → 50 chars) + Russian plural-month formatter (`formatMonths` с 1/2-4/5-10/11-14 правилами)

    - `src/lib/notifications/admin-initiated.ts` — `dispatchAdminInitiatedNotification` (3-channel: in-app via existing `createNotification`+`publishNotifications`, push via fire-and-forget `sendPushToUser`, Telegram via existing `telegram.send` queue job) + `processPlanEditedMassNotification` (batched 50/iteration с `Promise.allSettled`) + `enqueuePlanEditedMassNotification` (queue helper)
  - **Queue extension в `src/lib/queue/types.ts`:** новый `PlanEditedNotifyJob` type (`notification.billing.plan-edited.mass`) + payload validator + factory `createPlanEditedNotifyJob`. Worker handler `processPlanEditedNotifyJob` в `src/worker.ts`
  - **Integration sites (5):**
    - `plan-change.service.ts` — добавлен `BILLING_PLAN_GRANTED_BY_ADMIN` dispatch после tx commit (try/catch, failure logged but doesn't undo grant)
    - `cancel-subscription.service.ts` — **migrated** с generic `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED` → `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_BY_ADMIN`. Body теперь использует pure builder с accessUntil date
    - `billing/refund/route.ts` — добавлен `BILLING_PAYMENT_REFUNDED` dispatch после YooKassa refund (только при `refund.status === "succeeded"`). Defensive try/catch — refund уже произошёл externally, notification failure не должна surface как 500
    - `delete-review.service.ts` — добавлен `REVIEW_DELETED_BY_ADMIN` dispatch автору после tx commit. Резолв `targetName` через include `master.name` / `studio.provider.name`. Anonymous-author edge case не существует (`Review.authorId` non-nullable in schema)
    - `billing/plans/[id]/route.ts` PATCH — добавлена mass-fanout логика после tx commit: построение `PlanEditDiff` (name/isActive/prices), `buildPlanEditedSummary` (null если только sortOrder), `enqueuePlanEditedMassNotification` (worker рассылает в фоне). Sparse edits (только sortOrder) корректно skip enqueue

    - В транзакции: загружается `beforePlan` → если `inheritsFromPlanId` patched → cycle/parent validation → если `features` patched → build planNodes map с draft, `resolveEffectiveFeatures(effectiveInherits, ...)`, `assertRelaxedLimits(parsed, parentEffective)`

## КАК ИСПОЛЬЗОВАТЬ

1. **При обсуждении** новой фичи которую решаем не делать — **сразу** добавлять сюда
2. **Категория + приоритет** — обязательны
3. **Краткое описание** — что именно отложено + почему
4. **При выполнении** — перенести в «Выполнено» с датой
5. **Periodic review** — раз в спринт прогон списка, оценка приоритетов
6. **Перед каждым промптом** — проверить relevant section на things that might've been forgotten

Это **один источник правды** для отложенных задач. Не разбросано по разным промптам.

## ⚠️ ВАЖНО: ФАЙЛ ДОЛЖЕН БЫТЬ ЗАКОММИЧЕН

Этот файл — **проектная документация**, не локальное состояние. Должен быть в git:

```bash
# Если файл в .gitignore — убрать оттуда
# Открыть .gitignore и удалить строку с BACKLOG.md
# Затем:
git add BACKLOG.md
git commit -m "chore: add BACKLOG.md to repo (project documentation)"
```

Иначе при работе в эфемерных средах (Codespaces/Cursor Cloud/devcontainer) или после `git clean -fdx` файл пропадает. Это уже происходило один раз — потеряны были обновления из ADMIN-SHELL-A audit. Не повторять.
